1. PURPOSE AND EPISTEMIC BOUNDARY
================================================================
primary_validation_objective= test implementation equivalence between two independently authored implementations of the frozen V1.2 candidate specification on unseen NEW36 data, and test the emitted candidate artifact against a closed, pre-registered set of structural invariants.

this_protocol_does_not_establish= historical_generator_source_identity; historical_event_identity; uniqueness_of_the_specification; correctness_of_OLD36_informed_rule_choices; profitability; alpha; predictive_power; economic_significance; generalization_beyond_NEW36; live_trading_readiness; production_readiness

4. ARTIFACT CONTRACT
================================================================
artifact_pair= candidate_csv + candidate_manifest_json; reference_csv + reference_manifest_json
canonical_row_key= session_id; asset; axis; feature_family; variant_id; quantile; horizon_ms
row_key_must_be_unique=true
canonical_sort= session_order_from_APPENDIX_B; asset_order=BTC_then_ETH; axis_order=HZ_then_HG_then_HC_then_HB; feature_order=order_declared_in_APPENDIX_B; variant_order= HZ:T0_then_TZ; HG:G0_then_G1; HC:C0_then_C1; HB:B0_then_B1; quantile_order=0.80_then_0.90_then_0.95; horizon_order=1000_then_5000_then_30000; HZ_horizon_ms=NULL
csv_encoding=UTF-8 csv_line_terminator=LF null_serialization=empty_field float_serialization=repr(float) explicit_decimal_rounding=NONE
required_row_fields_ordered= protocol_version; session_id; asset; axis; feature_family; variant_id; quantile; horizon_ms; threshold_domain_finite_n; threshold_domain_nonzero_n; zero_n; zero_fraction; nonzero_unique_value_n; hz_discrimination_class; gate_zero_n; calculated_threshold; spacing_steps; pre_overlap_n; exact_spacing_pair_n; accepted_n; overlap_dropped_n; accepted_positions_sha256; mean_signed_bps; hit_rate; mean_abs_move

float_fields= quantile; zero_fraction; calculated_threshold; mean_signed_bps; hit_rate; mean_abs_move
axis_inapplicable_fields_must_be_NULL=true threshold_None_serializes_as_NULL=true metric_when_accepted_n_zero=NULL
accepted_positions_fingerprint= accepted grid_pos sorted ascending; cast each to signed little-endian int64; concatenate raw 8-byte representations without delimiter; SHA256; lowercase 64-character hexadecimal; empty set uses SHA256(empty_bytes)
exact_spacing_pair_definition= count consecutive pairs in the sorted pre-overlap positions array whose difference equals spacing_steps
IMPORTANT_exact_spacing_pair_definition= consecutive_pairs_only; not_all_unordered_pairs

any_S1_disagreement=IMPLEMENTATION_DISAGREEMENT S1_disagreement_grace_or_averaging=FORBIDDEN

7. APPENDIX A / S2 — CLOSED CANDIDATE INVARIANT LIST
================================================================
A01_INVENTORY= candidate emits exactly the closed Appendix-B inventory and 3456 rows.
A02_UNIQUE_KEY= canonical row key is unique.
A03_TZ_ZERO_EXCLUSION= TZ threshold domain is quality_admissible AND finite(signal) AND signal!=0.0.
A04_THRESHOLD_NONE= fewer than two values in the applicable threshold domain yields calculated_threshold=NULL and event N=0 for the corresponding threshold-dependent computation.
A05_HZ_COUNTS= finite_n>=nonzero_n>=0; zero_n=finite_n-nonzero_n; zero_fraction=NULL iff finite_n=0; otherwise zero_fraction=zero_n/finite_n and lies in [0,1].
A06_ZERO_FREE_CONTROL= if zero_n=0, T0 and TZ calculated_threshold must be equal within 1e-12.
A07_G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE= every G1 threshold equals the corresponding TZ depth_imbalance_l1 threshold for the same session,asset,q within 1e-12.
A08_G1_PARENT_SUBSET= G1 pre-overlap events are a subset of the parent depth_imbalance_l1 TZ gate.
A09_G1_RAW_SIGN= G1 confirmation uses raw external_ofi_consensus_l1 sign and raw bitget_depth_imbalance_l1 sign; z-score confirmation is forbidden.
A10_C1_GAP_THRESHOLD= C1 uses the frozen fair-gap threshold domain unchanged by HZ zero exclusion.
A11_C1_GAP_SUBSET= C1 pre-overlap events are a subset of the shared fair-gap threshold-crossing parent set.
A12_C1_RAW_SIGNS= C1 confirmation uses raw di_l1 and raw ext_ofi signs opposing gap; z_di_l1,z_ext_ofi,z_gap_d are forbidden from candidate confirmation.
A13_SPACING= k=horizon_ms//100; spacing_steps=max(10,k).
A14_B0= greedy earliest-first over ascending grid_pos; first event accepted; subsequent event accepted iff pos-last_accepted>=spacing_steps.
A15_B1_CONTROL= B1 is diagnostic only and uses strict pos-last_accepted>spacing_steps. B1 can never replace B0.
A16_COUNT_ACCOUNTING= pre_overlap_n=accepted_n+overlap_dropped_n.
A17_ACCEPTED_ORDER= accepted positions are unique, ascending and a subset of pre-overlap positions.
A18_FINGERPRINT= accepted_positions_sha256 follows the exact encoding in Section 4 and represents exactly accepted_n positions.
A19_I11= paired B1.accepted_n<=B0.accepted_n.
A20_I12= if exact_spacing_pair_n==0 then paired B0 and B1 accepted masks, accepted_n and accepted_positions_sha256 are identical.
A21_EXACT_SPACING_DEFINITION= exact_spacing_pair_n uses consecutive pairs in the sorted pre-overlap position array only.
A22_METRIC_EMPTY= accepted_n=0 implies mean_signed_bps=NULL, hit_rate=NULL and mean_abs_move=NULL.
A23_HIT_RATE= zero signed return counts as a miss.
A24_ASSET_INDEPENDENCE= BTC and ETH computations are independent.
A25_GOLDEN_ISOLATION= candidate and reference generators read no golden artifacts.
A26_OLD36_ISOLATION= candidate and reference generators read no OLD36 quantitative artifacts during V2 execution.
A27_COLLECTOR= collector SHA equals the frozen COLLECTOR_SHA256.
A28_FROZEN_ENGINE= FrozenAnalysisEngine remains NOT_CONFIGURED and accepts_input=false.
A29_PROTOCOL_IDENTITY= protocol SHA, candidate spec SHA and engine SHA in manifests match their frozen values.
A30_CONTROL_STATUS= T0,G0,C0,B1 remain diagnostic controls only and cannot trigger candidate substitution, tuning or a second candidate.
all_A01_through_A30_are_fail_closed=true any_A01_through_A30_violation=S2_FAIL
================================================================
8. S3 — SPECIFICATION-DERIVED INTERNAL IDENTITIES
================================================================
S3_1= gap-family diagnostic thresholds that normatively inherit the frozen fair-gap gate must equal the corresponding fair_gap_reversion threshold within 1e-12.
S3_2= a threshold defined without horizon dependence must remain identical across repeated diagnostic horizons for the same session,asset,feature,q within 1e-12.
S3_3= G1 threshold equals TZ depth_imbalance_l1 threshold. classification=IMPLEMENTATION_FIDELITY; not_independent_empirical_falsification.
S3_4= when HZ zero_n=0, T0 threshold equals TZ threshold. classification=IMPLEMENTATION_FIDELITY; not_independent_empirical_falsification.
S3_failure=S3_FAIL
================================================================
9. S4 — INVENTORY VALIDATION
================================================================
S4_requires= exact 12 NEW36 session IDs in frozen order; exact assets BTC,ETH; exact axes/features/variants/quantiles/horizons; exact per-axis counts; exact total 3456; no duplicate canonical keys; no undeclared rows; no missing rows
S4_failure=S4_FAIL
================================================================
10. S5 — DEGENERACY AND DIAGNOSTIC REPORTING
================================================================
hard_rules= threshold_NULL_implies_N_zero; accepted_n<=pre_overlap_n; counts_nonnegative; emitted_floats_finite_or_NULL
empirical_empty_or_sparse_cells= permitted; must_be_reported; do_not_by_themselves_fail_validation
no_empty_axis_expectation=WARNING_ONLY S5_hard_rule_violation=S5_FAIL
================================================================
11. REFERENCE IMPLEMENTATION INDEPENDENCE CONTRACT

reference_must_be_authored_before_any_NEW36_application_execution=true

amendment=001
status=NORMATIVE_CLARIFICATION
parent_protocol_sha256=5ed8a8b12726d395d25262dc3e4073250be3de94f3d62e64e99167921380ef95
candidate_spec_sha256=aed67b5ec9ae1b710ec3d96e1fcc2e15bd9c30fdd60e80cc68553d8fc3b597ca

purpose=
close reference-author determinacy ambiguities without changing the
frozen candidate selection or introducing NEW36-informed semantics

candidate_selection_unchanged=true
candidate_selection=HZ:TZ;HG:G1;HC:C1;HB:B0
diagnostic_controls_only=T0;G0;C0;B1

precedence=
this amendment clarifies the V2 artifact/control contract;
it does not modify the frozen candidate semantics;
where historical Stage3 diagnostic behavior differs from the frozen
candidate contract, the frozen candidate contract and V2 prevail

A1. PROTOCOL VERSION LITERAL

================================================================

protocol_version_literal=V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2

CSV protocol_version MUST equal exactly:
V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2

manifest protocol_version MUST equal exactly:
V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2

================================================================

A2. HZ / T0 CONTROL

================================================================

T0_role=DIAGNOSTIC_CONTROL_ONLY

For raw simple-feature signal s:

T0_threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(s)

TZ_threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(s) AND s!=0.0

T0_threshold=
type7_quantile(abs(s[T0_threshold_domain]),q)
if domain count>=2;
otherwise NULL

TZ_threshold=
type7_quantile(abs(s[TZ_threshold_domain]),q)
if domain count>=2;
otherwise NULL

HZ_is_threshold_only=true

HZ does NOT compute events, forward returns, overlap filtering or event metrics.

For BOTH T0 and TZ HZ rows:

horizon_ms=NULL
gate_zero_n=NULL
spacing_steps=NULL
pre_overlap_n=NULL
exact_spacing_pair_n=NULL
accepted_n=NULL
overlap_dropped_n=NULL
accepted_positions_sha256=NULL
mean_signed_bps=NULL
hit_rate=NULL
mean_abs_move=NULL

threshold_domain_finite_n=
count(T0_threshold_domain)

threshold_domain_nonzero_n=
count(TZ_threshold_domain)

zero_n=
threshold_domain_finite_n-threshold_domain_nonzero_n

zero_fraction=
NULL if threshold_domain_finite_n==0;
otherwise zero_n/threshold_domain_finite_n

nonzero_unique_value_n=
number of distinct Python-float values in
abs(s[TZ_threshold_domain]);
zero if TZ domain is empty

hz_discrimination_class exact literals and predicates:

if zero_n==0:
ZERO_FREE_CONTROL

else if T0_threshold != TZ_threshold using exact Python value equality
with NULL==NULL treated as equal:
HZ_DISCRIMINATING

else:
HZ_NONDISCRIMINATING_EQUAL_THRESHOLD

================================================================

A3. G0 CONTROL

================================================================

G0_role=DIAGNOSTIC_CONTROL_ONLY
G0_family=depthL1_extOFI

Define:

derived_domain=
quality_admissible
AND isfinite(z_di_l1)
AND isfinite(z_ext_ofi)

D=
(z_di_l1+z_ext_ofi)/2
on derived_domain

G0_threshold=
type7_quantile(abs(D[derived_domain]),q)

If fewer than 2 valid domain values:
G0_threshold=NULL
G0 pre-overlap set is empty

Otherwise:

G0_pre_overlap=
derived_domain
AND D!=0.0
AND abs(D)>=G0_threshold
AND isfinite(forward_return)

G0_direction=sign(D)

G0 overlap filter=B0 greedy >= spacing_steps

G0 calculated_threshold=G0_threshold

G0 fields:

threshold_domain_finite_n=NULL
threshold_domain_nonzero_n=NULL
zero_n=NULL
zero_fraction=NULL
nonzero_unique_value_n=NULL
hz_discrimination_class=NULL
gate_zero_n=NULL
exact_spacing_pair_n=NULL

spacing_steps,pre_overlap_n,accepted_n,overlap_dropped_n,
accepted_positions_sha256,mean_signed_bps,hit_rate,mean_abs_move
are computed.

================================================================

A4. C0 CONTROL

================================================================

C0_role=DIAGNOSTIC_CONTROL_ONLY
C0_family=gap_depth_extOFI

gap=bitget_gap_to_fair_bps

gap_threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(gap)

C0_threshold=
type7_quantile(abs(gap[gap_threshold_domain]),q)

D_ext=
(z_di_l1+z_ext_ofi)/2
where both z values are finite

C0_confirmation=
isfinite(D_ext)
AND D_ext!=0.0
AND sign(D_ext)==-sign(gap)

If C0_threshold is NULL:
C0 pre-overlap set is empty

Otherwise:

C0_pre_overlap=
gap_threshold_domain
AND isfinite(D_ext)
AND gap!=0.0
AND abs(gap)>=C0_threshold
AND D_ext!=0.0
AND sign(D_ext)==-sign(gap)
AND isfinite(forward_return)

C0_direction=-sign(gap)

C0 overlap filter=B0 greedy >= spacing_steps

C0 calculated_threshold=C0_threshold

C0 fields:

threshold_domain_finite_n=NULL
threshold_domain_nonzero_n=NULL
zero_n=NULL
zero_fraction=NULL
nonzero_unique_value_n=NULL
hz_discrimination_class=NULL
gate_zero_n=NULL
exact_spacing_pair_n=NULL

spacing_steps,pre_overlap_n,accepted_n,overlap_dropped_n,
accepted_positions_sha256,mean_signed_bps,hit_rate,mean_abs_move
are computed.

================================================================

A5. HB PRE-OVERLAP FAMILY MATRIX

================================================================

HB variants B0 and B1 operate on the SAME frozen baseline
pre-overlap set for each family.
Only the overlap inequality differs.

HB quantile=0.90

HB family=bitget_ofi:

signal=FEATURE_MAP[bitget_ofi]
domain=quality_admissible AND isfinite(signal)
threshold=type7_quantile(abs(signal[domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
domain
AND signal!=0.0
AND abs(signal)>=threshold
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=sign(signal)

This is the frozen baseline/T0 threshold domain.
It is NOT the TZ threshold domain.

HB family=depth_imbalance_l1:

signal=FEATURE_MAP[depth_imbalance_l1]
domain=quality_admissible AND isfinite(signal)
threshold=type7_quantile(abs(signal[domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
domain
AND signal!=0.0
AND abs(signal)>=threshold
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=sign(signal)

This is the frozen baseline/T0 threshold domain.
It is NOT the TZ threshold domain.

HB family=depthL1_extOFI:

derived_domain=
quality_admissible
AND isfinite(z_di_l1)
AND isfinite(z_ext_ofi)

D=(z_di_l1+z_ext_ofi)/2

threshold=type7_quantile(abs(D[derived_domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
derived_domain
AND D!=0.0
AND abs(D)>=threshold
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=sign(D)

HB depthL1_extOFI therefore uses the frozen G0/V1.1 baseline
pre-overlap semantics, NOT G1.

HB family=gap_depthL1:

gap=bitget_gap_to_fair_bps
di_l1=bitget_depth_imbalance_l1

threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(gap)

threshold=type7_quantile(abs(gap[threshold_domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
threshold_domain
AND isfinite(di_l1)
AND gap!=0.0
AND abs(gap)>=threshold
AND di_l1!=0.0
AND sign(di_l1)==-sign(gap)
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=-sign(gap)

HB family=gap_localOFI:

gap=bitget_gap_to_fair_bps
fair_ofi_alignment=bitget_fair_ofi_alignment

threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(gap)

threshold=type7_quantile(abs(gap[threshold_domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
threshold_domain
AND isfinite(fair_ofi_alignment)
AND gap!=0.0
AND abs(gap)>=threshold
AND fair_ofi_alignment==1.0
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=-sign(gap)

HB family=gap_depth_extOFI:

gap=bitget_gap_to_fair_bps

D_ext=
(z_di_l1+z_ext_ofi)/2
where both z values are finite

threshold_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(gap)

threshold=type7_quantile(abs(gap[threshold_domain]),0.90)

pre_overlap=
threshold_domain
AND isfinite(D_ext)
AND gap!=0.0
AND abs(gap)>=threshold
AND D_ext!=0.0
AND sign(D_ext)==-sign(gap)
AND isfinite(forward_return)

direction=-sign(gap)

HB gap_depth_extOFI therefore uses frozen C0/V1.1 baseline
pre-overlap semantics, NOT C1.

For every HB family:

spacing_steps=max(10,horizon_ms//100)

B0=
greedy earliest-first ascending grid_pos;
accept first;
subsequently accept iff
pos-last_accepted>=spacing_steps

B1=
same pre-overlap positions and direction;
greedy earliest-first;
subsequently accept iff
pos-last_accepted>spacing_steps

B1 remains diagnostic only.

================================================================

A6. EXACT-SPACING PRECEDENCE

================================================================

For V2, the normative definition is the frozen candidate definition:

exact_spacing_pair_n=
count of consecutive pairs in the sorted pre-overlap positions array
whose difference equals spacing_steps

Equivalent:

sum(
positions[i+1]-positions[i]==spacing_steps
for i in range(len(positions)-1)
)

The older Stage3 diagnostic implementation that counted all unordered
pairs at exact spacing is NOT normative for V2.

V2/candidate consecutive-pair semantics prevail.

================================================================

C1. mean_abs_move — INHERITED V1.1 METRIC SEMANTICS

================================================================

classification=
CLARIFICATION_OF_INHERITED_FROZEN_V1_1_METRIC_SEMANTICS

For every HG, HC and HB emitted row for which mean_abs_move is
defined as COMPUTED by Amendment 001:

mean_abs_move_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(forward_return)

mean_abs_move=
arithmetic mean of abs(forward_return) over
mean_abs_move_domain

The domain is evaluated independently for each session, asset and
horizon.

mean_abs_move is FEATURE-INDEPENDENT within the same
(session, asset, horizon).

It does NOT depend on:

threshold crossing
pre-overlap membership
accepted-event membership
variant
feature family
event direction

Therefore, where the same session, asset and horizon apply,
HG/G0, HG/G1, HC/C0, HC/C1 and all HB/B0/B1 rows share the same
mean_abs_move value.

If mean_abs_move_domain contains zero rows:

mean_abs_move=NULL

IMPORTANT:

The parent V2 generic rule
"metric_when_accepted_n_zero=NULL"
does NOT override this inherited feature-independent V1.1
mean_abs_move definition.

mean_signed_bps and hit_rate remain accepted-event metrics.
mean_abs_move is not an accepted-event metric.

This clause is a clarification of inherited frozen V1.1 semantics,
not a NEW36-informed rule and not a new candidate variant.

================================================================

C2. gate_zero_n — PROSPECTIVE SPECIFICATION COMPLETION

================================================================

classification=
PROSPECTIVE_SPECIFICATION_COMPLETION_BEFORE_NEW36

gate_zero_n is COMPUTED only for HG/G1 rows, as already required by
Amendment 001.

For HG/G1:

di_l1=bitget_depth_imbalance_l1

gate_finite_domain=
quality_admissible AND isfinite(di_l1)

gate_zero_n=
count(
quality_admissible
AND isfinite(di_l1)
AND di_l1==0.0
)

Equivalent:

gate_zero_n=
count of exact-zero di_l1 observations inside the finite
quality-admissible G1 gate population before TZ zero exclusion,
threshold crossing, external-OFI confirmation, forward-return
filtering, or overlap filtering.

Positive zero and negative zero are both equal to 0.0 and therefore
count as zero.

NaN and +/-infinity do not count.

gate_zero_n MUST NOT be computed from:

TZ_threshold_domain after zero exclusion
threshold-crossing events
pre-overlap events
accepted events
external_ofi confirmation

gate_zero_n is invariant across q and horizon for the same
session and asset.

This field is diagnostic metadata only.

It does NOT modify:

TZ threshold
G1 event mask
G1 direction
pre-overlap set
accepted set
event metrics
candidate selection

This exact definition is frozen prospectively before NEW36 access.

It MUST NOT later be represented as historically recovered behavior.

======================================================================
SuperBot V1.2
NEW36 VALIDATION PROTOCOL V2 — AMENDMENT 004
Document: V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2_AMENDMENT_004.txt

STATUS: DRAFT — NOT FROZEN
This document has no SHA256 identity. It SHALL NOT be cited as frozen,
and no SHA256 value SHALL be attributed to it, until it is frozen by the
protocol's own freezing procedure.
======================================================================

----------------------------------------------------------------------
0. KEY WORDS
----------------------------------------------------------------------

The key words "MUST", "MUST NOT", "SHALL", "SHALL NOT", "REQUIRED" and
"MAY" in this document are to be interpreted as normative requirements.
"Reference" means the independent reference implementation.
"Candidate" means the candidate application under validation.
"Implementation" means either of them.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
1. PURPOSE
----------------------------------------------------------------------

1.1 This Amendment closes exactly six determinacy gaps, identified
    before the independent reference implementation and designated
    R-01 through R-06.

1.2 This Amendment clarifies already-frozen semantics and the
    mechanical NEW36 input/output contract. It introduces no new
    scientific, quantitative or acceptance content.

1.3 This Amendment does NOT alter the scientific candidate, thresholds,
    event rules, overlap semantics, candidate composition, row
    inventory or acceptance criteria.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
2. FROZEN PARENT IDENTITIES
----------------------------------------------------------------------

2.1 Parent protocol: SuperBot V1.2 NEW36 Validation Protocol V2
    ("V2"), SHA256:
      5ed8a8b12726d395d25262dc3e4073250be3de94f3d62e64e99167921380ef95

2.2 Frozen collector identity, SHA256:
      e924edc1b8348d9cc8e74a37eaa17e6bbc80116de29d2f7a00cd43444d1265a3

2.3 Amendments 001, 002 and 003 are independently frozen normative
    documents with their own SHA256 identities as recorded at their
    freezing. This Amendment does not restate, recompute or modify
    those identities.

2.4 The engine allowlist already authorized under V2 and Amendments
    001/002/003 is unchanged by this Amendment.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
3. R-01 — FORWARD RETURN PRICE SERIES
----------------------------------------------------------------------

3.1 The array supplied as "canonical_mid" to

      _build_forward_return_array(canonical_mid, k)

    SHALL be exactly:

      grid["bitget_mid"].to_numpy(dtype=float)

    where "grid" is the canonical grid returned by
    build_canonical_grid() for the same (session_id, asset) block.

3.2 Consequently, for every position "pos" for which the forward
    return is valid:

      forward_return_bps[pos] =
          ln(bitget_mid[pos + k] / bitget_mid[pos]) * 10000

    where ln is the natural logarithm.

3.3 Validity of the endpoint and of the base, and the session-end
    rules, are exactly those already frozen. This section does not
    alter them. Where the frozen rules declare a forward return
    invalid, it SHALL remain invalid.

3.4 No other price series (including any other venue mid, trade price,
    microprice, bid, ask or derived fair value) SHALL be used as
    "canonical_mid".

3.5 This section is a clarification of frozen engine semantics, not a
    new quantitative choice.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
4. R-02 — fair_gap_reversion THRESHOLD DOMAIN
----------------------------------------------------------------------

4.1 The HZ zero-exclusion rule SHALL NOT alter the threshold domain of
    "fair_gap_reversion".

4.2 For "fair_gap_reversion", the threshold domain SHALL be exactly the
    set of positions satisfying:

      quality_admissible AND isfinite(raw_gap_signal)

4.3 Positions with raw_gap_signal exactly equal to zero ARE INCLUDED in
    the threshold domain and therefore participate in the frozen
    type-7 quantile calculation.

4.4 The TZ zero exclusion applies to EVENT ELIGIBILITY only. It SHALL
    NOT be applied when forming the threshold-domain quantile.

4.5 The same unchanged gap threshold-domain rule SHALL apply wherever
    the frozen C1 semantics consume the frozen fair-gap threshold.

4.6 This section implements, and does not extend, the already-frozen
    declarations:

      HZ_fair_gap_threshold_semantics_unchanged=true
      C1_gap_threshold=frozen_fair_gap_threshold_type7_quantile;domain_unchanged_by_HZ_zero_rule

4.7 This section SHALL NOT be interpreted as a new candidate rule.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
5. R-03 — NEW36 INPUT / LOADER CONTRACT
----------------------------------------------------------------------

5.1 Inventory. The frozen NEW36 inventory consists of exactly the 12
    session IDs listed in V2 Appendix B, in exactly that order. This
    Amendment does not restate, add, remove or reorder them.

5.2 Session representation. Each session is represented by its
    canonical session ZIP produced by the frozen collector identity
    declared in V2 (Section 2.2 above).

5.3 Logical identity only. This Amendment defines logical input
    identity. It does NOT define any archive naming convention,
    filesystem path, directory layout or deployment location. Such
    deployment details are non-normative and SHALL NOT affect outputs.

5.4 Processing requirements. The Reference and the Candidate SHALL:

    (a) process only the exact Appendix-B session IDs, and no others;
    (b) obtain the canonical parquet payload(s) from each session ZIP;
    (c) construct exactly one DataFrame block per (session_id, asset)
        for exactly the assets:
          BTC
          ETH
    (d) pass each asset block DataFrame to build_canonical_grid()
        before any quantitative computation;
    (e) preserve the frozen Appendix-B session order, and BTC-before-
        ETH asset order within each session, for canonical output
        ordering.

5.5 Asset resolution. For each canonical session ZIP and each
    requested asset, exactly "BTC" or "ETH", the implementation SHALL
    apply the frozen harness procedure:

    (a) enumerate the canonical "sync_grid_100ms" parquet payload
        entries of the session ZIP;
    (b) first, select the payload entries whose filename identifies the
        requested asset according to the frozen collector naming
        representation;
    (c) if no filename match exists for the requested asset, load the
        canonical "sync_grid_100ms" parquet payload entries and resolve
        the requested asset by exact equality on the parquet "asset"
        column:
          "BTC" for BTC
          "ETH" for ETH;
    (d) after parquet loading, each selected part SHALL be processed
        individually, in either selection path (b) or (c), as follows:

        A. If the part's DataFrame contains an "asset" column, exact
           row filtering SHALL be applied, equivalent to:

             df = df[df["asset"] == requested_asset].copy()

           Rows whose asset differs from the requested asset are
           dropped.

        B. If the part's DataFrame does NOT contain an "asset" column,
           the row-level asset filter is skipped for that part and the
           full DataFrame of that part is retained. Absence of the
           "asset" column by itself is NOT a structural failure.

        If, after processing and concatenating the selected parts
        (Section 5.7), the combined DataFrame has zero rows, the
        structural block-unavailable condition SHALL be raised. It is
        classified "FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID" and is never PENDING.

        If the combined DataFrame is non-empty, processing continues to
        build_canonical_grid(). No subsequent asset-column filter is
        performed by the engine, and none SHALL be added;
    (e) asset identity MUST NOT be guessed, inferred or reconstructed
        from quantitative feature values, price levels or any other
        quantitative content;
    (f) failure to deterministically construct the required BTC or ETH
        block is a structural application failure (Section 6).

    This procedure defines logical selection only; Section 5.3 applies
    and no deployment-specific absolute path is defined.

5.6 Absent quantitative feature columns. Where the frozen
    implementation contract treats a requested quantitative column as
    absent, the implementation SHALL represent that requested signal as
    an all-NaN float array whose length equals the canonical-grid
    length of the block. Quantitative observations SHALL NOT be
    synthesized, imputed, forward-filled, defaulted or otherwise
    fabricated. This section does not change which columns the frozen
    contract treats as absent.

5.7 Multiple parquet parts. A canonical session MAY contain multiple
    canonical "sync_grid_100ms" parquet parts for an asset. For each
    requested asset the implementation SHALL, per the frozen harness
    behavior:

    (a) determine the applicable canonical parquet parts under
        Section 5.5;
    (b) sort the selected part entries by filename in ascending
        order, where "filename" means the complete internal ZIP entry
        name/path string as returned by ZipFile.namelist() (NOT the
        basename), ordered by behavior equivalent to:

          sorted(selected_entry_names)

        with no "key=" function, using Python native ascending string
        ordering;
    (c) load them in that order;
    (d) combine them by row-wise concatenation equivalent to:
          pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)
    (e) only after that concatenation, pass the resulting asset
        DataFrame to build_canonical_grid().

    Where the authoritative frozen collector/session manifest declares
    the part inventory, the expected part count SHALL be validated
    against it. A required part SHALL NOT be silently skipped.

5.8 Part-order observability. The pre-concatenation part ordering of
    Section 5.7 is part of the deterministic loader contract. For any
    block successfully returned by build_canonical_grid(), however, it
    creates no observable quantitative ordering ambiguity:
    build_canonical_grid() performs a stable ascending sort by

      (local_ts_ms, sample_monotonic_ns)

    and an exact duplicate/tie on that two-field sort key is a
    CORRUPT_GRID hard failure. Every validly returned canonical grid
    therefore has unique two-field sort keys, and its canonical row
    order and grid_pos assignment are determined by those keys rather
    than by incoming parquet-part order. This section does not weaken
    or change the frozen CORRUPT_GRID behavior.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
6. STRUCTURAL APPLICATION FAILURE
----------------------------------------------------------------------

6.1 Each of the following SHALL be a structural application failure:

    (a) a required session ZIP is unavailable;
    (b) a canonical parquet payload is unavailable or unreadable;
    (c) asset identity cannot be deterministically resolved;
    (d) either the BTC or the ETH block required by the frozen
        inventory cannot be constructed;
    (e) build_canonical_grid() raises CorruptGridError;
    (f) a canonical grid is empty;
    (g) exact duplicate sort-key / full-row corruption is detected by
        the frozen canonical-grid rules;
    (h) a required canonical "sync_grid_100ms" parquet part is
        missing, unreadable, corrupt or inconsistent, including a part
        count inconsistent with the authoritative frozen
        collector/session manifest where that manifest declares the
        part inventory.

6.2 A structural application failure SHALL NOT be converted into a
    zero-row block, a NULL-result block, a skipped block, or any
    partial output presented as valid.

6.3 A structural application failure SHALL trigger the already-frozen
    V2 structural-failure behavior. This Amendment does not define,
    extend or replace that behavior.

6.4 Absence of a quantitative feature column handled under Section 5.6
    is NOT, by itself, a structural application failure.

6.5 Absence of the "asset" column in a selected parquet part is NOT, by
    itself, a structural application failure and does NOT, by itself,
    constitute an unresolved asset identity under Section 6.1(c); such
    a part is handled exactly as in Section 5.5(d)B. A combined asset
    DataFrame with zero rows under Section 5.5(d) is a structural
    application failure classified "FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID".

----------------------------------------------------------------------
7. R-04 — EVENT METRIC FORMULAS
----------------------------------------------------------------------

7.1 Metrics SHALL be calculated over ACCEPTED events only, i.e. after
    the applicable frozen overlap filter has been applied.

7.2 For each accepted event i:

      signed_return_i = direction_i * forward_return_bps_i

    where direction_i follows exactly the frozen family-specific
    direction semantics, which this Amendment does not redefine.

7.3 When accepted_n > 0:

      mean_signed_bps = arithmetic_mean(signed_return_i)

      hit_rate = count(signed_return_i > 0.0) / accepted_n

    An equivalent implementation of hit_rate is:

      mean(signed_return_i > 0.0)

7.4 A signed return exactly equal to zero is a MISS.

7.5 When accepted_n == 0:

      mean_signed_bps = NULL
      hit_rate        = NULL

7.6 This section does NOT alter the Amendment 003 semantics of
    "mean_abs_move" or of "gate_zero_n".

----------------------------------------------------------------------
8. R-05 — CSV BYTE SERIALIZATION
----------------------------------------------------------------------

8.1 Candidate and Reference CSV artifacts SHALL be serialized as
    follows:

    - encoding:           UTF-8, without BOM;
    - header row:         present, exactly one;
    - header fields:      exactly the frozen 25 fields, in frozen
                          order, with no additional fields;
    - index columns:      none;
    - delimiter:          ASCII comma ",";
    - quoting policy:     Python csv.writer default, QUOTE_MINIMAL;
    - quote character:    ASCII double quote (");
    - line terminator:    LF ("\n") for every record, including the
                          header;
    - CRLF:               forbidden;
    - NULL:               empty CSV field;
    - integer values:     base-10 integer strings;
    - float values:       Python repr(float), i.e. the repr of the
                          value as a Python built-in float;
    - explicit rounding:  none.

8.2 A FLOAT value SHALL first be represented as a Python built-in
    float and then serialized as:

      repr(float(value))

    Library-specific scalar repr strings (for example "np.float64(...)")
    SHALL NOT appear in the output. This is a clarification of the
    already-authorized "Python repr(float)" rule, not a new
    quantitative rule.

8.3 Row order is the frozen canonical output ordering, including the
    Appendix-B session order and BTC-before-ETH asset order stated in
    Section 5.4(e).

8.4 Under these rules the final output SHALL be a deterministic byte
    stream.

8.5 Non-finite FLOAT values. For every FLOAT field of the frozen
    25-field schema:

    - a non-NULL emitted value MUST be finite;
    - NaN is forbidden;
    - +inf is forbidden;
    - -inf is forbidden;
    - NULL remains the empty CSV field;
    - a non-finite value is NOT another representation of NULL.

    Non-finite values SHALL NOT be serialized as "nan", "inf", "-inf"
    or any other text. An emitted non-finite FLOAT is a validation
    failure under the already-frozen V2 comparison/validation rules.

8.6 Frozen field types. The frozen 25-field schema contains only the
    following already-frozen categories:

    STRING (8):
      protocol_version, session_id, asset, axis, feature_family,
      variant_id, hz_discrimination_class, accepted_positions_sha256

    INTEGER (11):
      horizon_ms, threshold_domain_finite_n,
      threshold_domain_nonzero_n, zero_n, nonzero_unique_value_n,
      gate_zero_n, spacing_steps, pre_overlap_n, exact_spacing_pair_n,
      accepted_n, overlap_dropped_n

    FLOAT (6):
      quantile, zero_fraction, calculated_threshold, mean_signed_bps,
      hit_rate, mean_abs_move

    No BOOLEAN field exists in the frozen 25-field schema. This
    section lists types only; header order remains the frozen order.
    NULL admissibility remains governed by the frozen NULL matrix and
    other frozen field-specific rules. This section creates no new NULL
    permission.

8.7 S1 FLOAT comparison and negative zero. No byte-level normalization
    of "-0.0" is imposed. The S1 FLOAT comparison remains the frozen
    rule: both NULL, OR both finite and

      abs(candidate_value - reference_value) <= 1e-12

    Accordingly "0.0" and "-0.0" are numerically equivalent for S1.
    CSV byte identity between Candidate and Reference is NOT an S1
    requirement. Role-specific CSV SHA256 values remain provenance
    identities and do not require Candidate/Reference CSV byte
    identity.

8.8 Floating-point evaluation order. This Amendment does not mandate
    "math.log" versus "numpy.log", any particular summation
    implementation, sequential versus pairwise summation, or any
    particular library's arithmetic-mean implementation. The normative
    requirements remain the frozen mathematical formulas, finite
    emitted FLOAT values (Section 8.5) and the frozen S1 absolute
    tolerance of 1e-12. Implementation-level floating-point choices
    are not independently frozen, provided emitted values satisfy the
    normative formulas and the S1 criteria.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
9. R-06 — MANIFEST HASH IDENTITIES
----------------------------------------------------------------------

9.1 The manifest field "collector_sha256" SHALL be the frozen
    collector identity literal:

      e924edc1b8348d9cc8e74a37eaa17e6bbc80116de29d2f7a00cd43444d1265a3

    It SHALL NOT be recomputed from any local collector file during the
    reference run.

9.2 The manifest field "protocol_sha256" SHALL be exactly the SHA256 of
    the frozen parent V2 protocol document:

      5ed8a8b12726d395d25262dc3e4073250be3de94f3d62e64e99167921380ef95

9.3 "protocol_sha256" SHALL NOT be:
    - a concatenated hash;
    - a Merkle or hash-tree value;
    - an aggregate hash of V2 plus any amendments;
    - the SHA256 of this Amendment 004;
    - dynamically recomputed from any modified protocol text.

9.4 Amendments 001, 002, 003 and 004 remain independently frozen
    normative documents with their own SHA256 identities. The value of
    the manifest field "protocol_sha256" nevertheless remains the
    parent V2 SHA256 stated in Section 9.2.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
10. NON-REGRESSION
----------------------------------------------------------------------

10.1 This Amendment resolves ONLY R-01 through R-06.

10.2 This Amendment does NOT change any of the following:

     - candidate composition;
     - HZ/HG/HC/HB definitions, except the explicit R-02
       clarification in Section 4;
     - feature families;
     - variants;
     - quantiles;
     - horizons;
     - threshold method;
     - G1 threshold source;
     - direction semantics;
     - overlap B0/B1 semantics;
     - exact-spacing consecutive-pair semantics;
     - accepted-position fingerprint encoding;
     - Amendment 003 "mean_abs_move";
     - Amendment 003 "gate_zero_n";
     - the canonical 3456-row inventory;
     - S1 comparison criteria;
     - the S2 invariant list;
     - the one-shot NEW36 policy;
     - the claim boundary and nonclaims.

10.3 No provision of this Amendment SHALL be read as filling any
     ambiguity outside R-01 through R-06.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
11. PRECEDENCE
----------------------------------------------------------------------

11.1 For ONLY the six subjects explicitly covered by this Amendment
     (R-01 through R-06), this Amendment is authoritative where
     earlier wording is incomplete or ambiguous.

11.2 Outside those six subjects, V2 and Amendments 001, 002 and 003
     remain unchanged and authoritative according to their existing
     precedence rules.

12.1 This Amendment was drafted without access to NEW36 data, NEW36
     results, OLD36, goldens, Stage1/Stage2/Stage3 artifacts or the

12.2 No provision of this Amendment was selected, tuned or informed by
     any NEW36 observation.

     NEW36 policy. NEW36 SHALL NOT be accessed as part of drafting,
     reviewing or freezing this Amendment.

======================================================================
1. PURPOSE AND SCOPE
======================================================================

1.1 The independent clean-room reference-authoring audit returned
    REFERENCE_SPEC_DETERMINACY=FAIL with exactly two blockers:

      R1: HZ "fair_gap_reversion" count/class semantics under A004 R-02.
      R2: Exact loader entry-selection / asset-selection /
          expected-part-count semantics.

1.2 This amendment resolves R1 and R2 and nothing else.

1.3 Every clause of this amendment carries exactly one of the following
    classifications:

    EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
      The clause states behavior already determined by the frozen
      specification (V2+A001..A004) and/or the frozen source code cited
      in 0.2. It introduces no new behavior. Where it corrects earlier
      wording, it corrects the DESCRIPTION of behavior, not the behavior.

    NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE
      The clause selects one resolution where existing evidence admits
      more than one. It MUST NOT be read or cited as pre-existing
      behavior.

1.4 This amendment contains exactly ONE NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE:
    Clause R1.3 ("fair_gap_reversion hz_discrimination_class diagnostic
    TZ-threshold interpretation"). All other clauses are
    EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR.

======================================================================
2. R1 — fair_gap_reversion HZ COUNT / CLASS SEMANTICS
======================================================================

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R1.1 NOTATION AND ACTUAL THRESHOLD (UNCHANGED)
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
----------------------------------------------------------------------

For "fair_gap_reversion" throughout this Section 2:

  s = raw_gap_signal

  T0_domain = quality_admissible AND isfinite(raw_gap_signal)
              (zero values ARE included)

  TZ_domain = quality_admissible AND isfinite(raw_gap_signal)
              AND raw_gap_signal != 0.0

The ACTUAL threshold used by "fair_gap_reversion" is, exactly as frozen
by A004 R-02:

  T0_threshold = type7_quantile(abs(raw_gap_signal[T0_domain]), q)

T0_threshold is the only threshold consumed wherever the frozen
fair-gap threshold is consumed by candidate or reference event
semantics. A005 does not modify T0_threshold, its domain, its quantile
method, or q.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R1.2 FIVE HZ COUNT / FRACTION FIELDS
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
----------------------------------------------------------------------

For "fair_gap_reversion" the following five HZ fields retain their
frozen A001 Section A2 definitions unchanged. A004 R-02 does not alter
them (A004 Section 10.2). They are restated here for determinacy only:

  (1) threshold_domain_finite_n
        = count(T0_domain)
        = count(quality_admissible AND isfinite(raw_gap_signal))

  (2) threshold_domain_nonzero_n
        = count(TZ_domain)
        = count(quality_admissible AND isfinite(raw_gap_signal)
                AND raw_gap_signal != 0.0)

  (3) zero_n
        = threshold_domain_finite_n - threshold_domain_nonzero_n

  (4) zero_fraction
        = NULL                                   if threshold_domain_finite_n == 0
        = zero_n / threshold_domain_finite_n     otherwise

  (5) nonzero_unique_value_n
        = number of distinct Python-float values in
          abs(raw_gap_signal[TZ_domain]);
        = 0 when TZ_domain is empty.

These five fields are NOT redefined by A005. Should any restatement
above be found to differ from A001 Section A2, A001 Section A2 governs
for these five fields.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R1.3 hz_discrimination_class
Classification: NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE
Identifier: "fair_gap_reversion hz_discrimination_class diagnostic
             TZ-threshold interpretation"
----------------------------------------------------------------------

R1.3.1 Reason a choice is required.
  A004 Section 4.4 states that the TZ zero exclusion SHALL NOT be
  applied "when forming the threshold-domain quantile". Existing
  evidence does not determine whether that prohibition also forbids
  computing a zero-excluding quantile solely for the A001
  hz_discrimination_class comparison (evidence audit Readings X and Y).
  Both readings are textually compatible with A004. This clause selects
  Reading X, preserving the diagnostic meaning of the A001 comparison.
  This selection is NOT pre-existing behavior.

R1.3.2 Diagnostic counterfactual threshold.
  SOLELY for computing hz_discrimination_class for "fair_gap_reversion",
  compute:

    TZ_diagnostic_threshold
        = type7_quantile(abs(raw_gap_signal[TZ_domain]), q)

  using the same q and the same type7_quantile definition as
  T0_threshold. Any empty-domain result of type7_quantile follows the
  existing frozen V2+A001..A004 semantics unchanged; A005 introduces no
  empty-domain rule.

R1.3.3 Diagnostic-only restriction.
  TZ_diagnostic_threshold:
    (a) MUST NOT replace, modify, or be substituted for T0_threshold;
    (b) MUST NOT be used for event eligibility, event acceptance,
        direction, overlap, spacing, or any quantitative event metric;
    (c) is consumed ONLY by the comparison in R1.3.4.
  A004 Section 4.4 continues to govern the ACTUAL threshold in full:
  the TZ zero exclusion is not applied when forming T0_threshold.

R1.3.4 Class rule (A001 rule, applied with the operands above).

    if zero_n == 0:
        hz_discrimination_class = ZERO_FREE_CONTROL
    else if T0_threshold and TZ_diagnostic_threshold differ under the
            exact A001 Section A2 Python-value comparison semantics:
        hz_discrimination_class = HZ_DISCRIMINATING
    else:
        hz_discrimination_class = HZ_NONDISCRIMINATING_EQUAL_THRESHOLD

  The comparison, including NULL handling (NULL == NULL treated as
  equal) and exact Python-value equality, is inherited verbatim from
  A001 Section A2 (lines 97-107). A005 does not restate, alter, or
  extend those comparison semantics. In the A001 rule, the operand
  "TZ_threshold" is, for "fair_gap_reversion", the
  TZ_diagnostic_threshold of R1.3.2; the operand "T0_threshold" is the
  actual threshold of R1.1.

R1.3.5 Explicit non-effects of R1.3.
  R1.3 introduces NO change to: the actual fair_gap_reversion
  threshold; its threshold domain; event eligibility (including the
  A004 event-eligibility TZ exclusion); accepted events; direction;
  overlap; spacing; or any quantitative event metric. R1.3 affects only
  the value of the diagnostic field hz_discrimination_class for
  "fair_gap_reversion".

======================================================================
3. R2 — EXACT sync_grid_100ms LOADER CONTRACT
======================================================================

All clauses in this Section 3:
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
Source: backend/recovery/harness.py _load_grid_for_block (lines 341-414)
and _get_block_summary (lines 418-463).

These clauses supply the literal predicate for the "frozen collector
naming representation" referenced in A004 Section 5.5(b).

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.1 PRIMARY ENTRY SELECTION
----------------------------------------------------------------------

For each requested asset string `asset`, with `zf` the opened session
ZIP:

  grid_names = sorted(
      n for n in zf.namelist()
      if "sync_grid_100ms" in n
      and n.endswith(".parquet")
      and asset in n
  )

Semantics:
  (a) `n` is the FULL internal ZIP entry path string exactly as returned
      by ZipFile.namelist(); it is NOT the basename.
  (b) `"sync_grid_100ms" in n` is case-sensitive Python substring
      containment anywhere in `n`.
  (c) `n.endswith(".parquet")` is a case-sensitive suffix test.
  (d) `asset in n` is case-sensitive Python substring containment of the
      requested asset string anywhere in the full path `n`, including
      directory components. It is NOT token matching, NOT path-component
      matching, and NOT basename-only matching. No word-boundary guard
      exists.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.2 FALLBACK ENTRY SELECTION
----------------------------------------------------------------------

If and only if the primary selection of R2.1 is empty:

  grid_names = sorted(
      n for n in zf.namelist()
      if "sync_grid_100ms" in n
      and n.endswith(".parquet")
  )

No filename/path asset filter is applied in the fallback. Predicates
(a)-(c) of R2.1 apply with identical semantics. Asset restriction in
the fallback case relies solely on the per-part row filter of R2.4.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.3 SORT
----------------------------------------------------------------------

In both R2.1 and R2.2 the ordering is Python built-in sorted() with no
key argument and default ascending order, applied to the complete
internal ZIP entry path strings (default Python str comparison). It is
NOT a basename sort and NOT a natural/numeric sort.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.4 PER-PART ROW FILTER
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Each selected entry is loaded, in the order of R2.3, into a DataFrame
`df`. Then, per part:

  if "asset" in df.columns:
      df = df[df["asset"] == requested_asset].copy()

  - The comparison is exact equality against the requested asset
    string (case-sensitive).
  - If the "asset" column is ABSENT, the row-level asset filter is
    skipped and the complete DataFrame for that part is retained.
    Absence of the "asset" column alone is NOT a structural failure
    (consistent with A004 Section 6.5).

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.5 MULTIPLE PARTS
----------------------------------------------------------------------

The per-part results of R2.4 are concatenated in the exact order of
R2.3:

  combined = pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)

before canonical grid construction. Canonical grid construction itself
is unchanged from V2+A001..A004.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.6 ZERO ROWS (frozen harness Case D)
----------------------------------------------------------------------

If `combined` has zero rows after the applicable filtering of R2.4
(including the case where no filter was applied because the "asset"
column was absent), the block is:

  FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID

and is NEVER classified PENDING. (Existing mechanism: raise
BlockDataUnavailableError, caught in _get_block_summary, yielding
BlockSummary(valid=False).)

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.7 NO ENTRY SELECTED (frozen harness Case B)
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
Source: backend/recovery/harness.py lines 380-386 (raise) and
        lines 451-458 (catch)
----------------------------------------------------------------------

R2.7.1 Trigger. If the primary selection (R2.1) is empty AND the
  fallback selection (R2.2), attempted as a consequence, is also empty,
  i.e. `grid_names` is empty after the fallback has been attempted:

    if not grid_names:
        raise BlockDataUnavailableError(...)   # frozen harness Case B

  Case B means: the RAW ZIP was opened successfully but no
  sync_grid_100ms parquet entry was found.

R2.7.2 Ordering. The Case B condition is raised BEFORE any per-part
  DataFrame list is created, BEFORE any parquet payload is opened or
  loaded, and BEFORE pd.concat is called. R2.4, R2.5 and R2.6 are not
  reached in this case.

R2.7.3 Classification. _get_block_summary catches
  BlockDataUnavailableError and returns BlockSummary(valid=False). The
  block is:

    FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID

  and is NEVER classified PENDING.

R2.7.4 Distinction from Case D (R2.6). Case B and Case D are distinct
  conditions:
    - Case B trigger: no entry selected after primary and fallback
      selection; raised before any part is loaded.
    - Case D trigger: one or more entries were selected and loaded, row
      filtering (where applicable) and concatenation were performed,
      and the combined DataFrame has zero rows.
  They have different trigger conditions and different raise sites /
  code paths. They share the same exception type
  (BlockDataUnavailableError), the same downstream handler, and the
  same final classification: FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID, never
  PENDING. Neither condition shall be treated as the other.

======================================================================
4. R2 — EXPECTED sync_grid_100ms PART COUNT
======================================================================

All clauses in this Section 4:
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
(including a correction of the DESCRIPTION given in A004 Section 5.7;
the behavior itself is unchanged)

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.8 SOURCE OF THE EXPECTED COUNT
----------------------------------------------------------------------

The expected sync_grid_100ms part count is:

  QARun.sync_grid_file_count          (backend/models.py line 98)

It is populated at ingest time (backend/qa_engine.py lines 248-274) as:

  len(list_dir_entries(inspection, "sync_grid_100ms"))

where list_dir_entries (backend/zip_security.py lines 182-190) returns
all NON-DIRECTORY ZIP entries whose path starts with "sync_grid_100ms/"
or contains "/sync_grid_100ms/". It is therefore an OBSERVED count of
all such files at ingest time; it is not restricted to ".parquet"
entries.

It is NOT a field extracted from the collector JSON manifest artifacts
(manifest.json, runtime_status.json, fast_collection_summary.json).
backend/manifest_parser.py FIELD_ALIASES and DERIVATIONS contain no
alias or derivation for it.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.9 CORRECTION OF A004 SECTION 5.7 WORDING
----------------------------------------------------------------------

A004 Section 5.7 describes the expected part inventory as that which
"the authoritative frozen collector/session manifest declares". That
description is inaccurate/imprecise. Wherever A004 Section 5.7 (or any
earlier text) refers to the collector/session manifest as the source of
the expected sync_grid_100ms part count, it SHALL be read as referring
to QARun.sync_grid_file_count as defined in R2.8.

The harness docstring at backend/recovery/harness.py lines 205-206
("parsed once at ingest time by qa_engine.py from the collector
manifest") is likewise inaccurate as a description; the code behavior
of R2.8 governs. This correction changes no behavior.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.10 VALIDATION WHEN THE EXPECTED COUNT IS AVAILABLE
Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
Source: backend/recovery/harness.py lines 201-278;
        backend/parquet_validator.py lines 73-82, 135-145, 227-371
----------------------------------------------------------------------

R2.10.1 Comparison. Where QARun.sync_grid_file_count is non-NULL, the
  frozen coverage validation (_authoritative_sync_grid_count ->
  _evaluate_sync_grid_coverage, harness.py lines 201-278) compares the
  sync-grid file count observed by the frozen reconstruction-time
  validation (sync_dir.files_seen, R2.10.2) against it. The condition
  is exact integer inequality:

    if QARun.sync_grid_file_count is not None
       and sync_dir.files_seen != QARun.sync_grid_file_count:
        HARD structural failure

  Any mismatch is a HARD structural failure.

R2.10.2 Observed count (sync_dir.files_seen), exact predicate.
  sync_dir is the PerDirResult for dataset directory "sync_grid_100ms"
  produced by parquet_validator.validate_all_parquet over the complete
  SESSION ZIP. For that ZIP, enumerate entries via ZipFile.infolist().
  Let `name` be the FULL internal ZIP entry path string of an entry
  (ZipInfo.filename; NOT the basename). An entry contributes exactly
  one to sync_dir.files_seen iff BOTH:

    (1) name.endswith(".parquet")
        (case-sensitive Python suffix test);
    AND
    (2) name.startswith("sync_grid_100ms/")
        or "/sync_grid_100ms/" in name
        (case-sensitive Python string semantics).

  Equivalently:

    sync_dir.files_seen = len([
        info for info in zf.infolist()
        if info.filename.endswith(".parquet")
        and (info.filename.startswith("sync_grid_100ms/")
             or "/sync_grid_100ms/" in info.filename)
    ])

  i.e. the number of ".parquet" entries under a "sync_grid_100ms/"
  directory component. (The frozen source additionally excludes names
  ending in "/"; no name satisfying (1) can end in "/", so the
  predicate above is exact.)

R2.10.3 Scope of the observed count. sync_dir.files_seen:
  (a) is SESSION-level: it is computed over the complete session ZIP;
  (b) is NOT asset-dependent: no requested-asset predicate participates
      in (1) or (2);
  (c) is determined independently of, and not by, the loader entry /
      asset selection of R2.1 and R2.2;
  (d) is a non-negative integer whenever sync_dir (the relevant
      PerDirResult) exists; it is never NULL in that case.

R2.10.4 Session-level evaluation. The coverage evaluation result is
  cached keyed by session_id (harness.py _part_coverage_cache). The
  expected-vs-observed comparison of R2.10.1 is therefore evaluated
  per session_id, and its result is reused for every requested asset
  of that session; it is not independently recomputed per requested
  asset. A005 states no other cache semantics.

R2.10.5 Expected vs observed predicates are NOT equivalent.
    EXPECTED  QARun.sync_grid_file_count (R2.8): ingest-time count of
              ALL non-directory entries under a "sync_grid_100ms/"
              directory component; NO extension restriction.
    OBSERVED  sync_dir.files_seen (R2.10.2): reconstruction-time count
              of ".parquet" entries ONLY under a "sync_grid_100ms/"
              directory component.
  The directory-component sub-predicate is the same on both sides; the
  ".parquet" restriction is present only on the observed side. Both
  predicates are codified as existing behavior and are NOT harmonized
  by A005: no filtering is added to either side and neither predicate
  is changed. A005 does not assert that the two counts are equal for
  any session; any inequality is handled solely by R2.10.1.

R2.10.6 Absent / zero sync_dir.
  Classification: EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_BEHAVIOR
  (NOT a NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE)
  Source: backend/recovery/harness.py _evaluate_sync_grid_coverage.

  (a) Guard. The frozen coverage evaluation contains the exact compound
      guard:

        if sync_dir is None or sync_dir.files_seen == 0:

      If the guard is true, the evaluator immediately returns:

        False, "sync_grid_100ms: no parquet parts present"

  (b) Single behavior. "sync_dir is None" (no PerDirResult for
      "sync_grid_100ms") and "sync_dir.files_seen == 0" are NOT
      distinct behaviors. They are the two operands of the same
      compound guard and produce exactly the same result.

  (c) Order. This guard is evaluated BEFORE:
        - sequence-validity checking;
        - corrupt-part / sync-failure checking;
        - the expected-count comparison of R2.10.1.

  (d) Consequence. When the guard fires, the expected-count comparison
      of R2.10.1 is NEVER reached, regardless of whether
      QARun.sync_grid_file_count is None, 0, or any other value.

  (e) Propagation. The caller propagates this result as
      FAILED / STRUCTURAL_INVALID, NEVER PENDING, with reason exactly:

        "sync_grid_100ms: no parquet parts present"

  (f) Control flow when expected_count == 0 (existing control flow
      only; no new rule):
        - sync_dir is None OR sync_dir.files_seen == 0:
            the early structural failure of (a) fires; expected-count
            equality is not tested, including when
            QARun.sync_grid_file_count == 0;
        - sync_dir.files_seen > 0 AND QARun.sync_grid_file_count == 0:
            the guard of (a) does not fire; the exact inequality
            comparison of R2.10.1 is reached; files_seen != 0;
            therefore the existing HARD structural mismatch failure of
            R2.10.1 applies.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
R2.11 VALIDATION WHEN THE EXPECTED COUNT IS UNAVAILABLE
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Where QARun.sync_grid_file_count is unavailable or NULL (session not
present in the database, or field not set), NO expected-count
comparison is performed. Unavailability alone is not a failure.

======================================================================
5. NON-REGRESSION
======================================================================

5.1 A005 changes or resolves ONLY:
    (1) the "fair_gap_reversion" HZ diagnostic count/class
        interpretation of Section 2;
    (2) exact sync_grid ZIP-entry selection, asset matching, fallback,
        and sorting (R2.1-R2.3);
    (3) row-level asset-filter behavior, insofar as needed for
        deterministic loader codification (R2.4-R2.6);
    (4) authoritative expected sync-grid count provenance and
        validation (R2.8-R2.11).

5.2 Everything else remains frozen exactly as V2+A001+A002+A003+A004.
    In particular the following are UNCHANGED:
      candidate composition; axes; feature families; variants;
      quantiles; horizons; actual thresholds (including the
      already-frozen A004 R-02 fair_gap_reversion rule); G1 threshold
      source; directions; B0/B1; spacing; fingerprint; A003 metrics;
      the 3456-row inventory; S1/S2; one-shot policy; claim boundary;
      A004 serialization/type rules; A004 forward return; actual
      fair_gap_reversion threshold and event semantics; the HZ
      definitions for all features other than "fair_gap_reversion";
      the HG/HC/HB definitions.

5.3 A005 introduces no new scientific rule, no new event rule, and no
    new metric.

======================================================================
6. PRECEDENCE
======================================================================

6.1 A005 prevails over earlier text (V2, A001, A002, A003, A004) ONLY
    to the extent necessary to resolve the matters enumerated in 5.1.

6.2 On every other matter, V2+A001+A002+A003+A004 remain unchanged and
    retain their existing internal precedence. A005 creates no general
    or residual precedence.

======================================================================
7. OBSERVATIONS RECORDED WITHOUT NORMATIVE EFFECT
======================================================================

The following are recorded for the independent determinacy auditor.
They create no rule and change no behavior.

  O-1 The primary asset predicate (R2.1(d)) is pure substring
      containment on the full path; an asset string may match entries
      belonging to other symbols whose paths contain it. This is
      existing behavior, codified as-is, not remediated.

  O-2 In the fallback path (R2.2), if a selected part lacks the "asset"
      column, all of its rows are retained (R2.4). This is existing
      behavior, codified as-is.

  O-3 The loader predicate ("sync_grid_100ms" substring + ".parquet"
      suffix) and the ingest-time count predicate (non-directory
      entries under a "sync_grid_100ms/" directory component, any
      extension) are not identical predicates. Both are codified as
      existing behavior; their relationship is governed by the frozen
      coverage validation of R2.10.

============================================================

1.1  A006 has exactly five functions:

     A. Codify the now-determinate EXISTING AMB-R3 behavior (sync-grid
        part-coverage structural validity) precisely enough that
        Candidate and an independently-authored Reference reach the same
        structural-validity decision (§4).

        source_sha256 with runtime HEAD binding (NNC-2, §6).

        including a normative pyarrow version, as a pre-application gate

     D. Require verified runtime uniqueness of sessions.session_id on the
        actual runtime database as a pre-application gate (NNC-4, §8).

     E. Define rejection of manifest JSON containing repeated object
        member names, for both roles (NNC-5, §8A).

     imperfect remains the normative behavior for this validation unless
     a prior frozen amendment explicitly says otherwise. Anomalies
     identified by the evidence are recorded in §4 as existing behavior.

1.3  A006 introduces no new normative choice within AMB-R3:

         A006_R3_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICES_COUNT=0

     NNC-3 and NNC-4 are pre-application runtime preconditions under
     which the codified AMB-R3 behavior executes. NNC-5 is a manifest
     serialization-validity rule. None of them changes any AMB-R3 rule,
     branch, string, counter or verdict.
