    3.3.3 TEST:
      backend/tests/test_v1_2_candidate.py
      backend/tests/test_v1_2_stage3.py
      backend/tests/test_v1_2_new36_candidate_app.py   NEW
      backend/tests/test_v1_2_new36_validators.py      NEW
      backend/tests/test_v1_2_new36_run.py             NEW

    3.3.4 CONTROL (provenance / firewall code, byte-bound):
      backend/recovery/v1_2_preflight.py
      backend/recovery/v1_2_acceptance.py
      backend/recovery/sandbox.py
      backend/recovery/allowlist.py

    3.3.5 Completeness rule. At Section 16 step 6 the implementer MUST
      record the set of modules under backend/ actually imported
      (directly or transitively) by the Candidate application, the
      Executors and the Orchestrator during the synthetic end-to-end run
      (sys.modules delta). Every such module MUST be a member of SOURCE
      or CONTROL; otherwise it MUST be added to SOURCE and step 6 is
      repeated. No execution-relevant imported module may remain outside
      the identity set. Paths under backend/recovery/reference/ MUST NOT
      appear in that delta (Section 5.4).

3.4 Aggregate algorithm (identical to v1_2_preflight.aggregate_sha256):
      for each path P in the set:
        rec := P + "\t" + lowercase_hex_sha256(raw working-tree bytes of P) + "\n"
      records sorted ascending by P (exact code-point string order);
      blob := concatenation of records, UTF-8;
      AGGREGATE := lowercase_hex_sha256(blob).
    Three aggregates:
      IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE_AGGREGATE_SHA256   over SOURCE ∪ ATTESTATION
      IMPLEMENTATION_TEST_AGGREGATE_SHA256     over TEST
      IMPLEMENTATION_CONTROL_AGGREGATE_SHA256  over CONTROL
    Every per-file SHA256 is also recorded individually (3.5).

3.5 Freeze record artifact (NOT created before Section 16 step 6):
      backend/recovery/specs/V1_2_CANDIDATE_IMPLEMENTATION_FREEZE_MANIFEST.txt
    Exact deterministic format: UTF-8, LF, no BOM, no trailing spaces,
    final LF, in this order and nothing else:
      FORMAT_VERSION=A008_FREEZE_MANIFEST_V1
      A008_FROZEN_PATH=backend/recovery/specs/V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2_AMENDMENT_008_DRAFT4.txt
      A008_FROZEN_SIZE_BYTES=<int>
      A008_FROZEN_SHA256=<64 hex>
      A008_FREEZE_COMMIT=<40 hex>
      CAPTURE_COMMIT=<40 hex>            (git rev-parse HEAD at capture)
      then one record per path, TAB-separated, ordered first by record
      type in the order SOURCE, TEST, ATTESTATION, CONTROL and within a
      type by relpath ascending:
      SOURCE<TAB><relpath><TAB><size_bytes><TAB><sha256>
      TEST<TAB><relpath><TAB><size_bytes><TAB><sha256>
      ATTESTATION<TAB><relpath><TAB><size_bytes><TAB><sha256>
      CONTROL<TAB><relpath><TAB><size_bytes><TAB><sha256>
      then:
      IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE_AGGREGATE_SHA256=<64 hex>
      IMPLEMENTATION_TEST_AGGREGATE_SHA256=<64 hex>
      IMPLEMENTATION_CONTROL_AGGREGATE_SHA256=<64 hex>
      REFERENCE_IDENTITY_OK=YES            (0.6 byte identities verified)
      PROTOCOL_IDENTITY_OK=YES             (0.2-0.4 verified)
    Every SHA256 is the raw-byte SHA256 of the committed blob at
    CAPTURE_COMMIT (git show <CAPTURE_COMMIT>:<relpath>) AND of the
    working-tree file; both MUST be equal. No value in this record may be
    guessed, pre-computed or copied from a draft; the record is written
    only at step 6 and frozen at step 8. The record file itself is not a
    member of any aggregate.

3.6 Eligibility rule. No implementation is NEW36-eligible until the
    record of 3.5 exists and is frozen (Section 16 step 8) and the final
    pre-application gate (4.12 gate P-03) verifies, in the interpreter
    that will execute the run, that every per-file SHA256 and every
    aggregate of 3.5, every 0.6 Reference identity and every 0.2-0.4
    protocol identity match exactly, and that `git rev-parse HEAD` is
    CAPTURE_COMMIT or a descendant in which every SOURCE/TEST/
    ATTESTATION/CONTROL blob is byte-identical (3.7). Any mismatch is
    PRECONDITION_FAIL; NEW36 is not consumed.

3.7 Provenance hazard (audit advisory N-6). The execution platform can
    create commits to task-state files (e.g. .emergent/*) that advance
    HEAD without any scientific source change. Therefore runtime_source_
    commit values in Candidate and Reference manifests are NOT required
    to equal CAPTURE_COMMIT or A008_FREEZE_COMMIT (A006 5.5); validity is
    established by byte identity of every execution-relevant file at the
    actual runtime HEAD (A006 6.5 predicate applied to every SOURCE/
    ATTESTATION/CONTROL path for the Candidate role, 12.2) and by the
    final gate of 3.6. A HEAD that differs from CAPTURE_COMMIT only by
    commits touching no path of 3.3 is conforming.

======================================================================
4. CANDIDATE NEW36 APPLICATION CONTRACT
======================================================================

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_03=CANDIDATE_APPLICATION_ENTRYPOINT_AND_CLI
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_04=COMPOSITION_RULE_AND_BINDING_FIELD_PROVENANCE
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_20=A008_INTRODUCED_SAFETY_RULES
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_21=CANDIDATE_PREAPPLICATION_GATE_ORDER

4.1 Location and nature. Exactly one new module
      backend/recovery/v1_2_new36_candidate_app.py
    It is a COMPOSITION/ORCHESTRATION layer. It MUST NOT define any
    threshold, domain predicate, mask, filter, direction, metric,
    fingerprint or count formula of its own; every canonical field MUST
    be obtained by calling the AUTHORIZED existing implementation named
    in the binding table of 4.7, exactly as stated there.

4.2 Invocation interface (A008-INTRODUCED DEPLOYMENT CONTRACT, 0.8(b);
    explicit invocation only; no import-time side effects; no FastAPI
    route):
      python -m recovery.v1_2_new36_candidate_app preflight
          --db <sqlite path> --environment-record <path>
          --session-zip-map <json path>
      python -m recovery.v1_2_new36_candidate_app generate
          --db <sqlite path> --environment-record <path>
          --session-zip-map <json path>
          --out-csv <path> --out-manifest <path> --out-evidence <path>
          --run-id <uuid> --ledger <path>
    The environment record is created by the Orchestrator (6.3), not by
    the Candidate application. `generate` MUST refuse (PRECONDITION_FAIL,
    nothing opened) unless the ledger (9.5) contains, as the LAST record
    for role CANDIDATE, a record with state=INVOKED and run_id equal to
    --run-id (9.7 step 5); this binds the Candidate to the Orchestrator.

4.3 Inputs.
    (a) --session-zip-map: JSON object {session_id: absolute path of the
        canonical session ZIP in the raw store} (A008-INTRODUCED
        DEPLOYMENT CONTRACT). Parsed with the NNC-5 parser (Section 8).
        Key set MUST equal exactly the 12 frozen NEW36 identifiers (14.2);
        extra, missing or repeated key => PRECONDITION_FAIL before any
        open.
    (b) --db: live runtime SQLite database, opened READ-ONLY (URI
        mode=ro). Used only for NNC-4 (Section 7) and the authoritative
        expected sync_grid_100ms part count (A005 R2.8, A006 4.10.2).
    (c) --environment-record: the shared NNC-3 record (6.3).
    (d) --out-csv, --out-manifest, --out-evidence: MUST NOT exist;
        created with exclusive-create ("xb"); pre-existence =>
        PRECONDITION_FAIL before any open.

4.4 Inventory. Session order = frozen Appendix-B order. The application
    MUST load checkpoint_registry.NEW36_SESSION_IDS and verify element-
    wise equality with the 12 literals of V2 Section 3 (also listed in
    14.2). Any difference => PRECONDITION_FAIL.

4.5 Data access and firewall.
    (a) Reads the raw store directly via 4.3(a); MUST NOT copy, move,
        re-download, re-transport, extract-to-disk or re-archive any ZIP;
        parquet payloads are read from the ZIP into memory only.
    (b) MUST NOT import recovery.sandbox directly and MUST NOT call
        recovery.sandbox.open_reference_zip, recovery.harness.
        _load_grid_for_block, recovery.harness._validate_sync_grid_part_
        coverage, or any function whose call chain reaches
        recovery.allowlist.assert_recovery_allowed with a NEW36 id.
        Transitive module import of recovery.harness through
        v1_2_diagnostics is permitted; no harness loader may be called.
    (c) MUST NOT modify, monkeypatch, wrap, disable or weaken
        recovery.allowlist.assert_recovery_allowed or checkpoint_registry.
        is_new36_registered. The recovery firewall remains fully active
        (CONTROL byte identity, 3.3.4). Preflight MUST verify, without
        opening data, that assert_recovery_allowed(NEW36_SESSION_IDS[0])
        raises PermissionError; otherwise PRECONDITION_FAIL.
    (d) frozen_engine.current_status() MUST report "NOT_CONFIGURED" and
        accepts_input False before and after the run.

4.6 Loader (by reference, unchanged). Per block (Appendix-B session
    order, BTC then ETH): obtain sync_grid_100ms parts exactly per A004
    5.4-5.8 and A005 R2.1-R2.7; session-level part coverage per A005
    R2.8-R2.10 and A006 4.9-4.11 using backend/parquet_validator.py
    functions and the DB-derived expected count; then
    engine.build_canonical_grid() (A004 5.4(d)); then the Candidate-side
    block preparation v1_2_diagnostics._prepare_block(session_id, asset,
    df) (which calls the frozen engine reconstruct_block for the block;
    no OLD36/golden access). Structural failures per A004 Section 6.

4.7 COMPOSITION RULE AND BINDING FIELD PROVENANCE (audit B-1).
    4.7.1 Stage3 row output is NOT normative. Any requirement to call
      v1_2_stage3._hz_block, _hg_block, _hc_block or _hb_block as
      authoritative row generators is WITHDRAWN. A Stage3 function MAY
      be reused ONLY as a sub-operation whose semantics are proven
      identical to the frozen rule for the specific field(s) named in
      the table; such reuse is listed explicitly below. Where a Stage3
      block function is reused as a sub-operation, ONLY the fields
      named "FROM <function>" may be taken from its output; every other
      field of that output MUST be discarded and composed per the table.
    4.7.2 Authorized existing implementations (exhaustive):
      ENGINE: engine.build_canonical_grid, engine._quality_admissible_mask
        (via _prepare_block), engine._quantile_type7,
        engine._build_forward_return_array, engine._greedy_overlap_filter
        (via v1_2_candidate.b0_greedy_filter), engine.GRID_MS,
        engine.FEATURE_MAP, engine.SIMPLE_FEATURES.
      CANDIDATE: v1_2_candidate.tz_threshold, g1_pre_overlap_mask,
        g1_parent_gate_mask, assert_g1_parent_subset, g1_direction,
        c1_pre_overlap_mask, c1_gap_parent_mask, assert_c1_gap_subset,
        c1_direction, spacing_steps_for_horizon, b0_greedy_filter,
        b1_diagnostic_filter, exact_spacing_pair_n, assert_hb_i11.
      DIAGNOSTICS: v1_2_diagnostics._prepare_block, _BlockContext,
        fingerprint_positions, _finite_mean_abs.
      STAGE3 (sub-operations only): _hz_block (fields listed in table),
        _hg_block (G0 rows only, fields listed), _hc_block (C0 and C1
        rows, fields listed), _hb_extract_pre_overlap (HB family
        pre-overlap sets), _compute_event_metrics (mean_signed_bps and
        hit_rate ONLY), _sha256_positions (equivalent to
        fingerprint_positions; either may be used).
      FORBIDDEN: v1_2_stage3.exact_spacing_pair_n (module-local, all
        unordered pairs; A001 A6 non-normative); v1_2_stage3._hb_block
        (does not expose positions; embeds forbidden helper);
        v1_2_stage3._hg_block output rows with variant_id G1 (wrong
        threshold source, F-2); the mean_abs_move value of any Stage3
        output row (F-4); the diagnostic_version field (F-5); any
        Stage3 output calculated_threshold for (fair_gap_reversion, TZ)
        (F-3).
    4.7.3 Binding table. Columns: FIELD | FROZEN NORMATIVE SOURCE |
      AUTHORIZED IMPLEMENTATION | STAGE3 STATUS | COMPOSITION RULE.
      Every row: COMPOSABLE_WITHOUT_QUANTITATIVE_PRIMITIVE_CHANGE=YES
      (justification: the AUTHORIZED IMPLEMENTATION already exists and
      is not modified).

      protocol_version | A001 A1 | constant literal
        "V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2" | NOT NORMATIVE (F-5:
        "diagnostic_version"="V1.2_STAGE3_FINAL") | emit the A001 A1
        literal as field 1; diagnostic_version MUST NOT appear.
      session_id | V2 §4, Appendix B | inventory of 4.4 | identical |
        pass-through in Appendix-B order.
      asset | A004 5.4(c) | ("BTC","ETH") | identical | BTC then ETH.
      axis | V2 §4 | literal per row family | identical | HZ,HG,HC,HB.
      feature_family | V2 §3 App.B, A002 B3 | STAGE3_HZ_FEATURES,
        STAGE3_HB_FAMILIES, "depthL1_extOFI", "gap_depth_extOFI"
        (verified equal to Appendix B) | identical | Appendix-B order.
      variant_id | V2 §4 | literals T0,TZ,G0,G1,C0,C1,B0,B1 | identical |
        per axis in V2 §4 variant_order.
      quantile | V2 §3 | (0.80,0.90,0.95); HB 0.90 | identical | as V2.
      horizon_ms | A001 A2 (HZ NULL), V2 §3 | (1000,5000,30000) |
        identical | HZ: NULL; HG/HC/HB: horizon.
      threshold_domain_finite_n | A001 A2 (count T0 domain) |
        FROM _hz_block (line-verified: quality & isfinite(signal)) |
        identical | HZ rows only; NULL elsewhere (A001 A7).
      threshold_domain_nonzero_n | A001 A2 / A005 R1.2 | FROM _hz_block
        (t0_domain & signal != 0.0) | identical | HZ only; NULL elsewhere.
      zero_n | A001 A2 | FROM _hz_block (finite_n - nonzero_n) | identical
        | HZ only.
      zero_fraction | A001 A2 | FROM _hz_block | identical | HZ only.
      nonzero_unique_value_n | A001 A2 | FROM _hz_block (distinct Python
        floats of abs(signal[TZ domain])) | identical | HZ only.
      hz_discrimination_class | A001 A2 lines 61-107; A005 R1.3 for
        fair_gap_reversion | FROM _hz_block (literals ZERO_FREE_CONTROL /
        HZ_DISCRIMINATING / HZ_NONDISCRIMINATING_EQUAL_THRESHOLD; operand
        comparison t0_thresh != tz_thresh with None==None equal; for
        fair_gap_reversion tz_thresh is numerically the A005 R1.3.2
        TZ_diagnostic_threshold) | identical | HZ only; NULL elsewhere.
      gate_zero_n | A003 C2 (HG/G1 only), A001 A7 | composed:
        count(quality & isfinite(di_l1) & di_l1 == 0.0) — the G1 path
        (F-2 rule below) computes it with the same predicate as
        _hg_block lines 695-697, proven identical | identical predicate |
        HG/G1 only; NULL on every other row.
      calculated_threshold | A001 A2 (HZ T0/TZ); A004 §4.1-4.4 + A005
        R1.1/R1.3.3(c) (fair_gap_reversion, F-3); A001 A07 + CANDIDATE_SPEC
        G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE=TZ (G1, F-2); A001 A3 (G0); A001 A4 + A004
        4.5 (C0/C1); A001 A5 (HB baseline) | HZ: FROM _hz_block EXCEPT
        (fair_gap_reversion, TZ) := value of the (fair_gap_reversion, T0,
        same q) row (F-3); G1: v1_2_candidate.tz_threshold(ctx.quality,
        ctx.columns["bitget_depth_imbalance_l1"], q) (F-2); G0: FROM
        _hg_block G0 row; C0/C1: FROM _hc_block; HB: threshold returned
        by _hb_extract_pre_overlap | NOT NORMATIVE for G1 (uses T0) and
        for (fair_gap_reversion, TZ) (uses zero-excluded diagnostic) |
        as stated; NULL policy: domain count < 2 => NULL (A001 A2, A8).
      spacing_steps | V2 A13 | v1_2_candidate.spacing_steps_for_horizon |
        identical (max(10, h//100)) | HG/HC/HB; HZ NULL.
      pre_overlap_n | A001 A3/A4/A5, A16 | G0: FROM _hg_block; G1:
        count(g1_pre_overlap_mask(...)) (F-2); C0/C1: FROM _hc_block;
        HB: len(positions) from _hb_extract_pre_overlap | identical
        except G1 (downstream of F-2) | as stated; HZ NULL.
      exact_spacing_pair_n | V2 §4, A001 A6, A21, A007.5 | v1_2_candidate.
        exact_spacing_pair_n(sorted pre-overlap positions, spacing_steps)
        | NOT NORMATIVE (F-1: Stage3 module-local all-pairs helper used
        by _hb_block) | HB rows only: positions from _hb_extract_pre_
        overlap (ascending by construction from ctx.gpos; the application
        MUST assert ascending order and MUST NOT re-sort silently);
        B0 and B1 rows of the same cell carry the same value; NULL on
        HZ/HG/HC (A001 A7).
      accepted_n | A14/A16/A17 | G1: v1_2_candidate.b0_greedy_filter over
        ctx.gpos[g1_mask]; HB: b0_greedy_filter (B0) and
        b1_diagnostic_filter (B1) over positions; G0/C0/C1: FROM
        _hg_block/_hc_block | identical (engine._greedy_overlap_filter
        >=; strict > for B1) | as stated; HZ NULL.
      overlap_dropped_n | A16 | pre_overlap_n - accepted_n | identical |
        composed arithmetic on the two emitted integers of the same row.
      accepted_positions_sha256 | V2 §4 encoding | v1_2_diagnostics.
        fingerprint_positions(accepted positions)["accepted_positions_
        sha256"] or _sha256_positions (byte-identical algorithm) |
        identical | G1 and HB: computed on composed accepted positions;
        G0/C0/C1: FROM _hg_block/_hc_block; HZ NULL.
      mean_signed_bps | A004 R-04 7.2-7.3 | _compute_event_metrics
        (directions, returns, accepted_mask)["mean_signed_bps"] |
        identical (mean(dir*ret); None when accepted_n==0) | G1/HB:
        computed on composed masks with directions g1_direction /
        _hb_extract_pre_overlap directions and returns from
        engine._build_forward_return_array(ctx.mid, k) (A004 R-01
        canonical_mid = bitget_mid via _prepare_block); G0/C0/C1: FROM
        _hg_block/_hc_block; HZ NULL.
      hit_rate | A004 R-04, A23 | _compute_event_metrics[...]["hit_rate"]
        | identical (mean(signed > 0.0); zero is a miss) | as above.
      mean_abs_move | A003 C1 | v1_2_diagnostics._finite_mean_abs(
        fwd[ctx.quality & np.isfinite(fwd)]) with fwd =
        engine._build_forward_return_array(ctx.mid, horizon_ms//GRID_MS)
        | NOT NORMATIVE (F-4: Stage3 _compute_event_metrics computes
        mean(abs(returns[accepted])) and None when accepted_n==0) |
        computed ONCE per (session_id, asset, horizon_ms) and written
        identically to every HG/HC/HB row of that triple, regardless of
        variant, feature or accepted_n; NULL only if the A003 domain is
        empty; HZ NULL.

    4.7.4 F-1 rule (exact_spacing_pair_n). The Candidate MUST obtain
      exact_spacing_pair_n from v1_2_candidate.exact_spacing_pair_n
      applied to the ascending pre-overlap positions array of the HB
      cell (as returned by _hb_extract_pre_overlap). It MUST NOT call
      v1_2_stage3.exact_spacing_pair_n and MUST NOT take the field from
      any _hb_block row. No all-unordered-pairs definition is allowed.

    4.7.5 F-2 rule (G1 threshold source = TZ). G1 rows MUST be composed
      entirely on the normative G1 path: tz_thr :=
      v1_2_candidate.tz_threshold(quality, di_l1, q) where di_l1 =
      ctx.columns["bitget_depth_imbalance_l1"] (the frozen parent source,
      A001 A07 / CANDIDATE_SPEC G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE=TZ); g1_mask :=
      g1_pre_overlap_mask(quality, di_l1, ext_ofi=ctx.columns["external_
      ofi_consensus_l1"], fwd, tz_thr); parent := g1_parent_gate_mask(
      quality, di_l1, fwd, tz_thr); assert_g1_parent_subset(g1_mask,
      parent) (abort = structural failure); positions := ctx.gpos[g1_mask]
      (ascending); directions := g1_direction(di_l1)[g1_mask]; returns
      := fwd[g1_mask]; accepted := b0_greedy_filter(positions, spacing);
      fingerprint, metrics per table. _hg_block G1 rows MUST be
      discarded. calculated_threshold of the G1 row = tz_thr, which by
      construction equals the (depth_imbalance_l1, TZ, q) HZ row value
      (A07 / S3_3). gate_zero_n per table.

    4.7.6 F-3 rule (fair_gap_reversion HZ/TZ). Classification:
      EXPLICIT_CODIFICATION_OF_EXISTING_FROZEN_SEMANTICS — not a choice.
      Derivation (unique): A004 4.1 "the HZ zero-exclusion rule SHALL NOT
      alter the threshold domain of fair_gap_reversion"; A004 4.4 "the TZ
      zero exclusion applies to EVENT ELIGIBILITY only. It SHALL NOT be
      applied when forming the threshold-domain quantile"; A005 R1.1
      "T0_threshold is the only threshold consumed wherever the frozen
      fair-gap threshold is consumed"; A005 R1.3.3(c) the zero-excluded
      TZ_diagnostic_threshold "is consumed ONLY by the comparison in
      R1.3.4"; V2 S3_1 gap-family thresholds must equal "the
      corresponding fair_gap_reversion threshold". The calculated_
      threshold of the (fair_gap_reversion, TZ, q) row is the threshold
      of that feature's TZ variant; that variant's threshold domain is,
      by A004 4.1/4.4, the unaltered domain quality & isfinite(gap) (zeros
      included); hence its value is T0_threshold (A005 R1.1), and
      emitting the zero-excluded quantile there would be a consumption
      forbidden by A005 R1.3.3(c). Therefore, for fair_gap_reversion:
        (a) the emitted canonical calculated_threshold of BOTH the T0 row
            and the TZ row = T0_threshold = type7_quantile(abs(gap[quality
            & isfinite(gap)]), q), NULL iff that domain count < 2;
        (b) the zero-excluded TZ_diagnostic_threshold is used ONLY as the
            operand of the hz_discrimination_class comparison (A005
            R1.3.4) and MUST NOT be emitted as calculated_threshold or
            consumed anywhere else;
        (c) all HZ count fields and hz_discrimination_class remain as
            produced by _hz_block (proven identical to A001 A2 / A005
            R1.2-R1.3.4).
      The other 11 HZ features are unaffected (A001 A2 generic TZ rule).
      D1_FAIR_GAP_RULE_UNIQUELY_DERIVED=YES.

    4.7.7 F-4 rule (mean_abs_move). Per table: FEATURE-INDEPENDENT per
      (session_id, asset, horizon_ms); domain quality_admissible AND
      isfinite(forward_return); mean of abs over that domain
      (v1_2_diagnostics._finite_mean_abs on fwd[ctx.quality &
      np.isfinite(fwd)]); NULL only if the domain is empty; MAY be
      non-NULL when accepted_n=0; MUST NOT inherit any Stage3
      accepted-event-conditioned value. (A003 C1; the V2 generic
      metric_when_accepted_n_zero=NULL rule does not apply to this field,
      as A003 C1 states.)

    4.7.8 F-5 rule (protocol_version). Field 1 of every row is
      protocol_version = "V1_2_NEW36_VALIDATION_PROTOCOL_V2" (A001 A1).
      diagnostic_version MUST NOT appear in the CSV.

    4.7.9 Fail-closed reuse. Stage3 sub-operations carry built-in
      drift guards (_assert_hz_t0_drift_guard, _assert_event_drift_guard,
      _assert_position_subset) that raise on inconsistency with the
      frozen engine metrics of the same block. Any such raise during
      NEW36 generation is a structural application failure under V2
      Section 13 (consumed, no retry). The application MUST NOT catch
      or suppress them.

    4.7.10 Frozen assertions. For every block the application MUST
      invoke assert_g1_parent_subset (G1), assert_c1_gap_subset (C1,
      with c1_gap_parent_mask over the frozen fair-gap threshold) and
      assert_hb_i11 (every paired HB cell) and MUST abort on any raise.
      It MUST NOT invoke any I12 / A20 check (A007). These assertions
      are safety aborts; they are NOT S2 evidence (Section 11).

4.8 Row schema and serialization. Exactly the 25 fields of V2 Section 4
    required_row_fields_ordered in that order; NULL/COMPUTED per A001 A7;
    V2 Section 4 serialization (UTF-8, LF, empty field for NULL,
    repr(float), no rounding); Python csv.writer with QUOTE_MINIMAL and
    lineterminator "\n" (codification: no frozen value contains ",",
    '"', CR or LF, so a conforming CSV contains no '"' character);
    canonical sort per V2 Section 4; key uniqueness verified before
    writing.

4.9 Expected inventory (unchanged): TOTAL=3456, HZ=1728, HG=432, HC=432,
    HB=864; 24 blocks. Any other count is a structural failure.

4.10 Manifest: Section 12. Evidence: Section 11.4.

4.11 A008_INTRODUCED_SAFETY_RULE (not inherited from A006):
    (a) CSV, manifest and evidence bytes are fully serialized in memory,
        hashed, then written with "xb"; after writing, each file is
        re-read and compared byte-for-byte to the serialized bytes; any
        difference is a structural failure.
    (b) runtime_source_commit and the Candidate source_sha256 object
        (12.2) are captured at gate P-05 and re-captured after generation
        before the manifest is serialized; any difference is a structural
        failure.
    Nothing is written before all 3456 rows exist and all checks pass.

4.12 Candidate pre-application gate order (deterministic; the first
    failing gate is the reported PRECONDITION_FAIL; none opens a ZIP):
      P-01 argument parsing; all --out-* paths absent (4.3(d))
      P-02 protocol identities 0.2-0.4 and A007 2.2 byte-verified
      P-03 implementation identities per 3.5/3.6 (freeze record present,
           every per-file SHA256 and aggregate equal; Reference 0.6;
           CONTROL byte identity)
      P-04 firewall self-check 4.5(c); FrozenAnalysisEngine 4.5(d)
      P-05 NNC-1 runtime_source_commit; source_sha256 object with HEAD
           binding (12.2)
      P-06 NNC-3 environment equivalence (Section 6)
      P-07 NNC-4 database uniqueness (Section 7), read-only
      P-08 inventory 4.4 and session-zip-map key set 4.3(a)
      P-09 input identity of the 12 mapped ZIPs (14.3: regular file,
           size, raw SHA256)
      P-10 ledger binding 4.2 (last CANDIDATE record INVOKED with --run-id)
    `preflight` runs P-01..P-09 (P-01 without --out-*, P-10 omitted).
    `generate` runs P-01..P-10 then begins application (first ZIP open).

    before any ZIP is opened. Failure = PRECONDITION_FAIL; not consumed.

    serialized per V2 Section 5 canonical JSON (UTF-8, sort_keys=true,

    unchanged to both roles.

6.4 The Candidate manifest informational block carries the recorded
    runtime_environment_identity_sha256 (12.4).

    repeated decoded member name makes the input INVALID; detection
    occurs before conversion into any ordinary mapping; first-wins /
    last-wins / silent collapse are FORBIDDEN; non-UTF-8 or unparseable

    sha256 recomputation; the evidence artifact records (11.4).

======================================================================
9. ONE-SHOT AUTHORIZATION, ORCHESTRATOR AND DURABLE LEDGER
======================================================================

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_09=NEW36_ONE_SHOT_AUTHORIZATION_AND_BOUNDARY
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_10=ORCHESTRATOR_AUTHORITY_AND_REFERENCE_LAUNCH_CONTRACT
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_11=DURABLE_LEDGER_SCHEMA_AND_STATE_MACHINE

9.1 Nature. No authorization mechanism exists in code or in V2/A001-A007
    (V2 Section 17). A008, ONCE FROZEN and ONLY after Section 16 through
    step 9, constitutes the authorization. DRAFT4 confers none.

9.2 Firewall NOT lifted. Authorization is granted by role to two named
    applications reading the raw store through the explicit map:
      CANDIDATE_MAX_NEW36_APPLICATION_INVOCATIONS=1
        backend/recovery/v1_2_new36_candidate_app.py generate
      REFERENCE_MAX_NEW36_APPLICATION_INVOCATIONS=1
        backend/recovery/reference/v1_2_reference.py generate (via 9.8)
    subject to all pre-application gates of that role passing, and ONLY
    when launched by the Orchestrator (9.3). Direct invocation of either
    `generate` outside the Orchestrator is a protocol violation; for the
    Candidate it is also technically refused (4.2 / P-10).

9.3 Orchestrator authority. backend/recovery/v1_2_new36_run.py is the
    sole authority controlling both role invocations. It is a member of
    SOURCE (3.3.1). It performs Section 9.7. The frozen Reference is
    launched as a subprocess through its existing public interface (9.8)
    and is NOT modified.

9.4 Consumption boundary (NARROWING of V2 Section 13, never widening).
    A role is CONSUMED when the Orchestrator has DURABLY appended the
    INVOKED record for that role (9.6). This is strictly earlier than or
    equal to V2 Section 13's "first quantitative read". Consequently a
    role is treated as consumed even if a crash occurs after the durable
    INVOKED record and before the child process opens any ZIP. The V2
    Section 13 infrastructure-retry exception remains available ONLY for
    failures occurring strictly before the durable INVOKED record.
    Static checks, stat(), raw-byte SHA256 of ZIP files, DB metadata
    reads, environment-identity construction and synthetic tests do NOT
    consume.

9.5 Ledger. Persistent path (under /app; never solely in /var/tmp or any
    ephemeral location):
      backend/recovery/reports/v1_2_new36/one_shot_ledger.jsonl
    Operational evidence, not scientific input. Append-only JSON Lines;
    one record per line; each line = canonical JSON (UTF-8, ensure_ascii
    =true, sort_keys=true, separators (",",":")) followed by exactly one
    LF; parsed with the NNC-5 parser. No delete, reset, truncate,
    rewrite or reorder operation exists in the controlled runner; any
    such operation on the ledger invalidates the run.

9.6 Ledger record schema (EXACTLY these members, all required; null only
    where stated):
      "schema_version": "A008_LEDGER_V1"
      "run_id": UUIDv4 string identifying the validation attempt (R3;
          exact semantics and reuse-on-restart rule in the note below
          and in 9.11)
      "role": "CANDIDATE" | "REFERENCE"
      "state": "GATES_PASS" | "INVOKED" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED"
      "utc": ISO-8601 UTC timestamp "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.ffffffZ" (non-normative metadata)
      "runtime_head": 40-hex `git rev-parse HEAD` at record time
      "runtime_environment_identity_sha256": 64-hex (6.3)
      "input_inventory_id": "NEW36_12SESSIONS_24BLOCKS_3456ROWS_V1" (A002 B1)
      "a008_sha256": 64-hex frozen A008 identity
      "freeze_manifest_sha256": 64-hex raw SHA256 of 3.5 record file
      "output_artifact_sha256": null, or (COMPLETED only) object
          {"csv": 64-hex, "manifest": 64-hex, "evidence": 64-hex|null}
          (evidence null for REFERENCE)
      "detail": null, or (FAILED only) short string classification:
          "STRUCTURAL" | "INFRASTRUCTURE" | "INCOMPLETE_AFTER_RESTART" | "OTHER"
    "run_id" semantics (R3): the run_id is a UUIDv4 string identifying
    the validation attempt; it is generated once for a fresh attempt
    (9.7 step 1) and, upon any Orchestrator restart, is reused unchanged
    for the remainder of the SAME attempt exactly per 9.11; it MUST NOT
    be regenerated for an attempt that already has a durable ledger
    record for either role.
    Durable append semantics (each record): open with O_APPEND (and
    O_CREAT on first use); ONE complete write of the full line;
    flush; fsync(file descriptor); fsync(parent directory descriptor)
    where the platform supports it; only after these return may the
    Orchestrator proceed. A record is "durable" only after this
    sequence completes.

9.7 Orchestrator procedure (ordered; each step fail-closed):
     1. Determine the validation-attempt run_id per 9.11 (R3): a fresh
        UUIDv4 for a fresh attempt, or the existing recovered run_id
        when 9.11 rule 2 applies; read existing ledger (if any) and
        apply the recovery rules 9.9 BEFORE anything else; if either
        role is already consumed, that role is skipped permanently.
     2. Create the shared environment record (6.3) with "xb" if absent
        (if present, validate it and reuse it; a record whose identity
        differs from the current interpreter's identity is
        PRECONDITION_FAIL).
     3. Verify input identity of the 12 mapped ZIPs (14.3) and all
        protocol/implementation identities (3.6); write nothing to the
        ledger on failure.
     4. Run the Candidate `preflight` (4.12 P-01..P-09) as a subprocess;
        on success durably append {role:CANDIDATE, state:GATES_PASS}.
     5. Durably append {role:CANDIDATE, state:INVOKED} (role now
        consumed, 9.4); then launch the Candidate `generate` subprocess
        with --run-id and --ledger; on exit code 0 and successful
        sealing (raw SHA256 of the three outputs computed by the
        Orchestrator and equal to the values inside the manifest),
        durably append COMPLETED with output_artifact_sha256; otherwise
        durably append FAILED (detail STRUCTURAL or INFRASTRUCTURE per
        available evidence; no retry).
     6. GATE (R2, narrows V2 Section 13, never widens): before any
        Reference action, verify from the durable ledger that the
        latest terminal record for role CANDIDATE in this validation
        attempt (9.11 run_id) is COMPLETED.
          - If the latest Candidate record is FAILED: the validation
            attempt STOPS here; the Reference `preflight` MUST NOT be
            run; no {role:REFERENCE, state:INVOKED} record MUST be
            appended; the Reference role remains unconsumed for this
            attempt.
          - If a Candidate INVOKED record exists without a later
            COMPLETED or FAILED record: the Orchestrator MUST first
            apply the recovery rules of 9.9 to the Candidate role and
            re-evaluate; the Reference MUST NOT proceed until the
            Candidate terminal state is COMPLETED.
          - This gate narrows, and does not merge, the independent
            consumption states of 9.4/9.9.
        R2_REFERENCE_REQUIRES_CANDIDATE_COMPLETED=YES.
        Only once Candidate=COMPLETED: run the Reference `preflight`
        through 9.8 as a subprocess; on success durably append
        {role:REFERENCE, state:GATES_PASS}.
     7. Durably append {role:REFERENCE, state:INVOKED}; then launch the
        Reference `generate` through 9.8; seal and append COMPLETED or
        FAILED as in step 5 (evidence null).
     8. Only if BOTH roles are COMPLETED: run S1 then S2 (Sections 10,
        11) on the sealed artifacts; write s1_report.json, s2_report.json
        ("xb").
     9. Write the execution evidence index (14.4) including the raw
        SHA256 and size of the ledger file at that moment.
    Steps 4-7 are strictly sequential; roles are never concurrent. The
    Candidate-then-Reference order is fixed by A008 for determinacy of
    the ledger; it has no scientific meaning.

9.8 Reference launch contract (A008-INTRODUCED DEPLOYMENT CONTRACT; the
    Orchestrator enforces it; the Reference is unchanged):
    (a) Command (subprocess, same interpreter as the Orchestrator, cwd =
        repository root, module path backend/ on sys.path exactly as the
        Reference's own tests require):
          python backend/recovery/reference/v1_2_reference.py preflight
              --db <db> --environment-record <shared record 6.3>
          python backend/recovery/reference/v1_2_reference.py generate
              --db <db> --environment-record <shared record 6.3>
              --session-zip-map <the SAME map file as the Candidate>
              --out-csv <14.4 reference_new36.csv>
              --out-manifest <14.4 reference_new36_manifest.json>
    (b) Reference input isolation (R1; replaces the DRAFT2 cwd-based
        rule; does NOT require Candidate artifacts to be physically
        invisible from the repository-root cwd). The Reference receives
        ONLY: the read-only DB path, the shared environment record, the
        session-zip map, and two fresh output paths. The Reference MUST
        NOT receive Candidate CSV, Candidate manifest, Candidate
        supplemental evidence, S1/S2 reports or the ledger through:
        argv; stdin; environment variables; config/input parameters;
        explicit path arguments; or imported data/module dependencies.
        Mere filesystem visibility of a Candidate artifact under the
        repository-root cwd is NOT "receiving" and is not, by itself, a
        violation of this clause. The Orchestrator MUST NOT pass any
        Candidate artifact path to the Reference and MUST NOT instruct
        the Reference to read any Candidate artifact. The Orchestrator
        MUST launch the Reference with a minimal environment (PATH,
        PYTHONPATH as required, LANG/LC_ALL=C.UTF-8, no other
        variables) and MUST verify the Reference output paths do not
        exist before launch. R1_REFERENCE_INPUT_ISOLATION_CLOSED=YES.
    (c) The Orchestrator MUST NOT read Reference source to construct
        (a); (a) is fully specified here.
    (d) The Reference's own gates (NNC-1..NNC-4 as implemented in the
        frozen Reference) remain its own; the Orchestrator additionally
        enforces 9.7 steps 3 and 6 before INVOKED.

9.9 Recovery rules (applied at 9.7 step 1 from the durable ledger, per
    role independently):
      A. No INVOKED record for the role: role NOT consumed; no process
         was launched; the run may proceed for that role.
      B. INVOKED durably recorded, crash before the child's first
         quantitative read: role CONSUMED (9.4); no retry.
      C. Crash after first quantitative read: CONSUMED; no retry.
      D. INVOKED present without a later COMPLETED or FAILED record for
         the same role: CONSUMED and FAILED/INCOMPLETE; the Orchestrator
         durably appends {state:FAILED, detail:"INCOMPLETE_AFTER_RESTART"}
         and NEVER relaunches that role.
      E. COMPLETED present: consumed successfully; artifacts sealed.
      F. FAILED present: consumed; no retry.
    Candidate and Reference states are independent: consumption or
    failure of one neither consumes nor authorizes the other. A
    GATES_PASS record alone does not consume.

9.10 Multiple INVOKED records for one role, any record after COMPLETED/
    FAILED for that role other than nothing, a run_id mismatch between
    the INVOKED record and the Candidate --run-id, a Candidate COMPLETED
    and a Reference COMPLETED record for the same attempt bearing
    different run_id values, any ambiguous or incompatible set of active
    run_ids across roles for what should be a single attempt (9.11, R3),
    or an unparseable ledger line are ledger corruption: fail closed,
    nothing launched, manual audit required; no reset.

9.11 ONE VALIDATION ATTEMPT = ONE RUN_ID (R3; closes recovery semantics
    left open by DRAFT2). A validation attempt has exactly one run_id.
    Candidate and Reference artifacts compared by S1 MUST belong to the
    same validation attempt and the same run_id.
    Rules:
      1. Fresh attempt: the Orchestrator creates one UUIDv4 run_id
         (9.7 step 1).
      2. Candidate COMPLETED, Reference not yet INVOKED, then the
         Orchestrator process restarts: the Orchestrator recovers and
         reuses the Candidate's existing run_id from the durable
         ledger. It MUST NOT create a new run_id for the Reference or
         for the remainder of the attempt.
      3. Candidate COMPLETED and Reference COMPLETED: S1 (10.2)
         requires both ledger records to carry the same run_id before
         any field comparison.
      4. Candidate FAILED: the attempt terminates; the Reference is not
         invoked (9.7 step 6, R2).
      5. Candidate INVOKED then crash: recovery (9.9 rule D) appends
         FAILED/INCOMPLETE_AFTER_RESTART using that SAME run_id; the
         Reference remains uninvoked.
      6. Ambiguous or incompatible active run_ids, or Candidate and
         Reference COMPLETED with different run_ids: LEDGER_CORRUPTION
         (9.10); fail closed; launch nothing; manual audit required.
    Execution evidence / S1 precondition (10.2) MUST verify:
      candidate_run_id == reference_run_id == validation_run_id
    The Candidate manifest a008_provenance.run_id (12.4) remains
    unchanged in shape and meaning. The Reference manifest schema
    (A002 B2) is NOT modified by this rule; Reference run_id binding is
    external, through the ledger and the execution evidence index
    (14.4), never through the Reference manifest.
    R3_VALIDATION_ATTEMPT_RUN_ID_CLOSED=YES.

======================================================================
10. S1 EXECUTOR CONTRACT
======================================================================

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_12=S1_COMPARATOR_EXECUTION_CONTRACT

10.1 Location: backend/recovery/v1_2_new36_validators.py, command
     `s1 --candidate-csv --candidate-manifest --reference-csv
      --reference-manifest --ledger --out-report`. Explicit invocation
     (by the Orchestrator, 9.7 step 8).

10.2 Sealed inputs only. Before comparison: compute raw SHA256 and size
     of the four artifacts; require csv_sha256/csv_size_bytes in each
     manifest to equal the computed values of its CSV; parse each
     manifest with the NNC-5 parser; recompute manifest_precomparison_
     sha256 (V2 Section 5) and require equality; require both COMPLETED
     ledger records' output_artifact_sha256 to equal the computed
     values; require the run_id of the COMPLETED CANDIDATE ledger record
     to equal the run_id of the COMPLETED REFERENCE ledger record (9.11,
     R3) — candidate_run_id == reference_run_id == validation_run_id.
     Any failure => s1_status "INVALID_INPUT". No artifact is modified.

10.3 CSV lexical contract (both roles): UTF-8; no BOM; LF only; no CR
     anywhere; exactly one header line equal to the 25 V2 field names in
     order, comma-separated; every row exactly 25 fields; no '"'
     character anywhere (4.8); final byte LF; no blank line. Deviation
     => INVALID_INPUT.

10.4 Key = (session_id, asset, axis, feature_family, variant_id,
     quantile, horizon_ms), compared as EXACT SERIALIZED STRINGS
     (including the serialized quantile lexeme and the empty lexeme for
     NULL horizon_ms); no numeric normalization of key lexemes. Both
     files: keys unique (A02); row count exactly 3456; key sets equal;
     canonical sort order equal. Every row compared; no sampling.

10.5 Field comparison (V2 Sections 4 and 6):
     string fields: exact serialized equality;
     integer fields (horizon_ms, threshold_domain_finite_n,
       threshold_domain_nonzero_n, zero_n, nonzero_unique_value_n,
       gate_zero_n, spacing_steps, pre_overlap_n, exact_spacing_pair_n,
       accepted_n, overlap_dropped_n): either both empty (NULL), or both
       match ^-?[0-9]+$ and are equal as integers; a non-empty lexeme not
       matching ^-?[0-9]+$ (e.g. "1000.0") => INVALID_INPUT for that
       file;
     float fields (quantile, zero_fraction, calculated_threshold,
       mean_signed_bps, hit_rate, mean_abs_move): both NULL, or both
       parse (Python float()) to finite values and abs(c-r) <= 1e-12;
       NaN, +Inf, -Inf lexemes or non-parsable lexemes => INVALID_INPUT;
     accepted_positions_sha256: exact lowercase 64-hex equality (and
       both match ^[0-9a-f]{64}$ when non-NULL).
     No adaptive or relative tolerance; no rounding.

10.6 Manifest comparison: all V2 Section 5 semantic fields exactly equal
     (A002 B2 shapes), except the role-specific provenance fields
     implementation_role, runtime_source_commit, source_sha256,
     csv_sha256, csv_size_bytes, manifest_precomparison_sha256, each
     validated for shape/validity per its own rules. Informational keys
     (12.4) are ignored for S1 (A002 B5).

10.7 Report (canonical JSON per 9.5 line rules but indent=2 and final
     LF; "xb"): s1_status ("PASS"|"FAIL"|"INVALID_INPUT"); row counts;
     key_set_mismatch_n; sort_mismatch (bool); field_mismatch_total;
     field_mismatch_by_field (object, 25 keys, ints); manifest_mismatch_
     fields (array); mismatches (array of {key, field, candidate,
     reference} in canonical key order, ALL mismatches); input identities
     (four SHA256 + sizes). Any mismatch => FAIL (IMPLEMENTATION_
     DISAGREEMENT). Deterministic for identical inputs.

======================================================================
11. S2 EXECUTOR CONTRACT, OBSERVABILITY MATRIX AND SUPPLEMENTAL EVIDENCE
======================================================================

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_13=S2_VALIDATOR_EXECUTION_CONTRACT_AND_MATRIX
    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_14=CANDIDATE_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_ARTIFACT

11.1 Location: backend/recovery/v1_2_new36_validators.py, command
     `s2 --candidate-csv --candidate-manifest --candidate-evidence
      --freeze-manifest --ledger --out-report`. Reads ONLY the sealed
     Candidate CSV, manifest and evidence, the frozen 3.5 record and the
     hash-bound Candidate source (STATIC mode). It MUST NOT read any
     Reference artifact and MUST NOT open any NEW36 ZIP:
     S2_REQUIRES_POSTHOC_NEW36_REREAD=NO. It is not a third NEW36
     application.

11.2 Invariant set: exactly A01-A19 and A21-A30 (A007). A20 appears in
     the report ONLY as the fixed member WITHDRAWN_INVARIANTS=["A20"];
     no predicate, handler or replacement exists for it (A007.7). A19
     is active and fail-closed. The rule "a completed artifact implies
     PASS" (DRAFT1 GENERATION_TIME_ASSERTION) is DELETED: every
     invariant is evaluated from observable evidence per 11.3.

11.3 BINDING OBSERVABILITY MATRIX. Columns: INVARIANT_ID |
     REQUIRED_EVIDENCE | EVIDENCE_SOURCE | EVALUATION_MODE |
     POSTHOC_NEW36_REREAD_REQUIRED. Sources: CANDIDATE_CSV (CSV),
     CANDIDATE_MANIFEST (MAN), CANDIDATE_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE (EVD),
     STATIC_HASH_BOUND_SOURCE (STATIC). Float comparisons use the 10.5
     float rule.
      A01 | exact Appendix-B key inventory, 3456 rows, per-axis counts | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A02 | canonical key unique | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A03 | TZ domain = quality & finite & != 0 | STATIC (v1_2_candidate.tz_threshold and _hz_block TZ predicate, hash-bound) + CSV (nonzero_n <= finite_n) | STATIC+ARTIFACT | NO
      A04 | threshold NULL <=> domain count < 2, and NULL => N=0 | CSV for HZ (finite_n / nonzero_n; fair_gap TZ uses finite_n per 4.7.6) and for NULL=>pre_overlap_n=accepted_n=0 on HG/HC/HB; EVD threshold_domain_n for every HG/HC/HB cell (partial evidence) | ARTIFACT+EVIDENCE | NO
      A05 | HZ count identities and zero_fraction range | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A06 | zero_n=0 => T0 and TZ thresholds equal within 1e-12 | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A07 | every G1 threshold = (depth_imbalance_l1,TZ,q) threshold within 1e-12 | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A08 | G1 pre-overlap positions ⊆ parent gate positions | EVD (pre_overlap_positions ⊆ parent_positions of G1 records) | EVIDENCE | NO
      A09 | raw-sign confirmation; no z-score in G1 | STATIC (AST: g1_pre_overlap_mask is the only G1 mask source; CANDIDATE_FORBIDDEN_CONFIRMATION_COLUMNS never read in the app) | STATIC | NO
      A10 | C1 threshold = fair_gap_reversion T0 threshold (S3_1) | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A11 | C1 pre-overlap ⊆ gap parent set | EVD (C1 records) | EVIDENCE | NO
      A12 | C1 raw opposing signs; z_* forbidden | STATIC (c1_pre_overlap_mask only source; forbidden columns absent) | STATIC | NO
      A13 | spacing_steps = max(10, horizon_ms//100) | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A14 | B0 greedy earliest-first, >= spacing | EVD (recompute b0 rule over pre_overlap_positions; equals accepted_positions of B0 record) + CSV accepted_n | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT | NO
      A15 | B1 strict >; B1 never candidate | EVD (recompute strict rule; equals B1 accepted_positions) + CSV (variant_id B1 only as control) + STATIC | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT+STATIC | NO
      A16 | pre_overlap_n = accepted_n + overlap_dropped_n | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A17 | accepted positions unique, ascending, ⊆ pre-overlap | EVD (accepted_positions vs pre_overlap_positions) + CSV counts | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT | NO
      A18 | accepted_positions_sha256 = V2 §4 encoding of exactly accepted_n positions | EVD accepted_positions re-hashed vs CSV field; len = accepted_n | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT | NO
      A19 | paired B1.accepted_n <= B0.accepted_n | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO   (ACTIVE)
      A21 | exact_spacing_pair_n = consecutive pairs of sorted pre-overlap with diff == spacing | EVD (recompute over pre_overlap_positions) vs CSV field | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT | NO
      A22 | accepted_n=0 => mean_signed_bps, hit_rate NULL (mean_abs_move governed by A003 C1, checked for feature-independence per (session,asset,horizon)) | CSV | ARTIFACT | NO
      A23 | hit_rate = count(sign==+1)/accepted_n; zero is a miss | EVD accepted_signed_return_signs vs CSV hit_rate (1e-12) | EVIDENCE+ARTIFACT | NO
      A24 | BTC/ETH independence | STATIC (per-block composition; no cross-asset state in app) | STATIC | NO
      A25 | no golden reads | MAN golden_artifacts_read=false + STATIC (v1_2_preflight.verify_no_golden_imports_in_candidate extended to the app module by the validator, forbidden tokens) | MANIFEST+STATIC | NO
      A26 | no OLD36 quantitative reads | MAN old36_quantitative_data_read=false + STATIC (no sandbox/harness loader call) | MANIFEST+STATIC | NO
      A27 | collector_sha256 = frozen | MAN | MANIFEST | NO
      A28 | frozen_analysis_engine_state / accepts_input | MAN | MANIFEST | NO
      A29 | protocol_sha256, candidate_spec_sha256, engine_sha256 = frozen | MAN | MANIFEST | NO
      A30 | T0/G0/C0/B1 present only as controls; exactly one candidate variant per axis; no extra variants | CSV + MAN variant_sets_per_axis | ARTIFACT+MANIFEST | NO
     Supplemental-evidence invariants: A04 (partial), A08, A11, A14,
     A15, A17, A18, A21, A23. STATIC mode means: the validator hashes the
     Candidate application source file, requires equality with the 3.5
     record, and evaluates the predicate on the AST of that hash-bound
     source; it never executes it.

11.4 CANDIDATE SUPPLEMENTAL EVIDENCE ARTIFACT.
     11.4.1 Nature. Candidate-only; NOT part of the canonical 25-field
       CSV (CANONICAL_SCHEMA_CHANGED=NO); ignored by S1; never emitted
       by the Reference; emitted DURING the single Candidate invocation
       from the same in-memory block state used for the CSV rows (no
       second read of any ZIP); sealed immediately (4.11(a)); consumed
       by S2 only.
     11.4.2 Path/name (14.4): candidate_new36_evidence.jsonl
     11.4.3 Records. One record per HG, HC and HB canonical row (1728
       records), in the SAME canonical order as the CSV rows of those
       axes (V2 Section 4 canonical_sort restricted to axes HG,HC,HB).
       No HZ records (HZ has no events; A04/A05/A06 for HZ are CSV-
       observable).
     11.4.4 Record schema (EXACTLY these members; member order in the
       serialized line is sort_keys=true):
         "session_id": string
         "asset": string
         "axis": "HG"|"HC"|"HB"
         "feature_family": string
         "variant_id": "G0"|"G1"|"C0"|"C1"|"B0"|"B1"
         "quantile": float (repr, same lexeme as CSV)
         "horizon_ms": int
         "spacing_steps": int
         "threshold_domain_n": int  — count of the threshold domain from
             which this cell's calculated_threshold was computed (G0:
             derived-domain count; G1: TZ depth_imbalance_l1 domain
             count; C0: its derived domain; C1: fair_gap T0 domain count;
             HB: the family's baseline threshold-domain count)
         "pre_overlap_positions": array of int (ascending grid_pos of
             the cell's pre-overlap events; for B1 records: null — the
             array is identical to the paired B0 record of the same
             (session_id, asset, feature_family, quantile, horizon_ms))
         "parent_positions": array of int (ascending) for variant_id G1
             (positions of g1_parent_gate_mask) and C1 (positions of
             c1_gap_parent_mask); null for every other variant
         "accepted_positions": array of int (ascending; exactly the
             positions whose fingerprint is the CSV accepted_positions_
             sha256 of this row)
         "accepted_signed_return_signs": array of int in {-1,0,1}, same
             length and order as accepted_positions; value =
             sign(direction_i * forward_return_bps_i) with exact 0 for a
             zero signed return (A004 R-04 operands; A23)
       All integers are JSON integers; no floats other than quantile; no
       NaN/Inf anywhere.
     11.4.5 Serialization. UTF-8; no BOM; each record = canonical JSON
       (ensure_ascii=true, sort_keys=true, separators (",",":")) + one
       LF; LF only; deterministic record order 11.4.3; final byte LF.
       Parsed by the NNC-5 parser line by line.
     11.4.6 Sealing and manifest recording (A002 B5 extension
       mechanism, informational only, ignored by S1):
         candidate_evidence_size_bytes = byte length of the file
         candidate_evidence_sha256     = raw SHA256 of the file
       both recorded under the manifest informational block (12.4) and
       in the ledger COMPLETED record (9.6).
     11.4.7 Minimality. The evidence contains only what 11.3 requires.
       No metric values, no thresholds, no raw feature values are
       emitted. S2 MUST verify, for every record, consistency with the
       CSV row of the same key (pre_overlap_n = len(pre_overlap_
       positions) or of the paired B0 record; accepted_n =
       len(accepted_positions)); any inconsistency is S2 FAIL.
     11.4.8 Storage precondition. Because position arrays scale with
       event counts, the Orchestrator MUST verify at 9.7 step 3 that the
       filesystem holding 14.4 has at least 1073741824 free bytes;
       otherwise PRECONDITION_FAIL (nothing consumed). See the
       INFORMATIVE note after Section 14.

11.5 Report (canonical JSON, indent=2, "xb"): s2_status ("PASS"|"FAIL"
     |"INVALID_INPUT"); evaluated_invariants (exactly the 29 ids
     A01-A19, A21-A30 in order); WITHDRAWN_INVARIANTS=["A20"];
     failed_invariant_count; failed_invariant_ids; per-invariant entries
     {id, evidence_source, evaluation_mode, status, detail_count,
     details[]}; s5 (informative degeneracy counts; S5 hard-rule
     violations reported as s5_status separately, V2 Section 10). Any
     failed invariant => FAIL.

12. MANIFEST / PROVENANCE CONTRACT (CANDIDATE)

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_15=CANDIDATE_SOURCE_SHA256_SET_AND_INFORMATIONAL_BLOCK

12.1 Required key set: exactly V2 Section 5 required_manifest_keys
     (unchanged). Values by existing normative definition (none
     redefined): protocol_version (A001 A1); implementation_role
     "CANDIDATE"; runtime_source_commit (A006 5.6: `git rev-parse HEAD`,
     stripped, ^[0-9a-f]{40}$, at generation time); csv_sha256 /
     csv_size_bytes (raw CSV bytes); manifest_precomparison_sha256 (V2
     Section 5); session_ids, assets, axes, feature_sets_per_axis,
     variant_sets_per_axis, quantiles_per_axis, horizons_per_axis,
     expected_row_counts_per_axis, actual_row_counts_per_axis,
     expected_total_row_count, actual_total_row_count (A002 B2/B3/B6);
     collector_sha256 (A004 9.1 literal, not recomputed); engine_sha256,
     candidate_spec_sha256 (V2 Section 2 literals); protocol_sha256
     (A004 9.2, parent V2 SHA256 only); new36_inventory_id (A002 B1);
     golden_artifacts_read=false; old36_quantitative_data_read=false;
     frozen_analysis_engine_state="NOT_CONFIGURED";
     frozen_analysis_engine_accepts_input=false;
     fingerprint_encoding_definition (A002 B4 literal);
     quantile_method="TYPE7_LINEAR"; grid_ms=100.

12.2 source_sha256 (Candidate). Semantics unchanged from A006 6.9 (JSON
     object mapping repository-root-relative paths to raw-byte lowercase
     SHA256 of working-tree files at generation time). A008 fixes the
     path set for the NEW36 Candidate application to EXACTLY SOURCE ∪
     ATTESTATION (3.3.1 + 3.3.2, twelve keys), superseding for this
     application only the two-path Stage3 set described parenthetically
     in A006 6.9 (the Stage3 manifest's own historical set is unchanged).
     HEAD binding: each path MUST exist at runtime_source_commit and its
     committed blob bytes MUST hash equal to the working-tree value (A006
     6.5 predicate applied to the Candidate role). Candidate and
     Reference source_sha256 remain role-specific, not cross-role
     equality fields (A006 6.9).

12.3 Serialization: V2 Section 5 (UTF-8 JSON, sort_keys=true, indent=2,
     final newline); manifest_precomparison_sha256 computed with that
     member set to null, then written, then serialized again.

12.4 Informational block (A002 B5: "If an implementation emits
     additional informational keys outside the required schema, the
     frozen comparator MUST ignore them for S1"; never compared
     cross-role; MUST NOT be added to the Reference manifest). The
     Candidate manifest MUST carry the single object member
     "a008_provenance" with EXACTLY:
       "runtime_environment_identity_sha256": 64-hex (Section 6)
       "implementation_source_aggregate_sha256": 64-hex (3.4)
       "implementation_test_aggregate_sha256": 64-hex
       "implementation_control_aggregate_sha256": 64-hex
       "freeze_manifest_sha256": 64-hex (raw SHA256 of 3.5 record file)
       "a008_sha256": 64-hex (frozen A008 identity; unknown to this draft)
       "amendment_identities": object {"A001":…,"A002":…,…,"A007":…} 64-hex each
       "candidate_evidence_size_bytes": int (11.4.6)
       "candidate_evidence_sha256": 64-hex (11.4.6)
       "run_id": UUIDv4 string (9.6)

13. SYNTHETIC PRE-NEW36 TESTABILITY
======================================================================

    A008_NEW_NORMATIVE_CHOICE_16=SYNTHETIC_CONFORMANCE_CONTRACT

13.1 Before NEW36 eligibility, the Candidate application, the Executors
     and the Orchestrator MUST be exercised end-to-end WITHOUT any real
     NEW36 quantitative byte, under pytest, with no network, no real DB,
     no path under backend/data/, no path under 14.4. Tests live in the
     files of 3.3.3.

13.2 Permitted synthetic inputs: in-memory/temporary ZIPs containing
     synthetic sync_grid_100ms parquet parts with the frozen column set;
     a temporary isolated SQLite DB reproducing the sessions/qa_runs/
     raw_files schema incl. the unique index on sessions.session_id;
     synthetic session metadata; synthetic canonical rows; a temporary
     ledger path. Synthetic session identifiers MUST NOT be any of the
     12 frozen NEW36 identifiers except where a test verifies inventory
     enforcement by supplying the frozen id LIST against synthetic ZIP
     bytes (identifiers are not quantitative data). Real NEW36 ZIP paths
     MUST NOT be referenced by any test. Synthetic runs do NOT consume
     NEW36.

13.3 Retained minimum coverage (each an explicit test):
     (a) exact 25-field schema/header order; protocol_version literal;
         diagnostic_version absent;
     (b) manifest serialization (sort_keys, indent, final newline,
         precomparison round-trip, A002 B2 shapes, a008_provenance block);
     (c) NNC-5: repeated member at top level, nested object, object in
         array => rejection; well-formed accepted; non-UTF-8 rejected;
     (d) inventory enforcement: 11, 13, reordered, duplicated, wrong-id
         maps => PRECONDITION_FAIL before any open (counting resolver);
     (e) NNC-3 success; altered distributions / python version /
         requirements sha / pyarrow => fail before open;
     (f) NNC-4 duplicate rows => fail; missing unique index => fail;
         read-only enforcement;
     (g) Candidate generation path over 12 synthetic sessions x 2 assets
         => 3456 rows, per-axis counts, canonical sort, key uniqueness,
         "xb" refusal, byte re-read check, evidence emitted and sealed;
     (h) F-1 precedence: HB exact_spacing_pair_n is the consecutive-pair
         count on synthetic positions where all-pairs and consecutive
         counts differ (e.g. [0,5,10], spacing 10: consecutive=0,
         all-pairs=1);
     (i) S1 PASS fixture; S1 mismatch fixtures: string, integer, float
         diff > 1e-12 fails, float diff exactly 1e-12 passes, NULL vs
         value, key-set difference, sort difference, manifest semantic
         difference, role-specific difference ignored, informational
         keys ignored;
     (j) every S2 handler A01-A19, A21-A30 with a passing and a failing
         fixture; report enumerates exactly 29 ids;
     (k) A20/I12 absence: static scan of new modules for "A20", "I12",
         "I_HB_I12" outside the WITHDRAWN_INVARIANTS literal and
         comments => none; A19 handler present and failing on
         B1.accepted_n > B0.accepted_n;
     (l) firewall: assert_recovery_allowed(NEW36 id) raises after
         importing the app; recovery.sandbox not in sys.modules after
         a synthetic generate; no harness loader called (call counter).

13.4 Added tests (audit findings T-1..T-9):
     T-1 S1: "nan", "inf", "-inf", "NaN", "Infinity" lexemes in any
         float field => INVALID_INPUT; also in key/integer fields.
     T-2 Ledger crash persistence (simulated aborts via injected
         failure points): (i) abort before durable INVOKED => recovery
         rule A (not consumed, relaunch permitted); (ii) abort after
         durable INVOKED before the child process opens any synthetic
         ZIP => rule B (consumed; relaunch refused); (iii) abort after
         the child began without terminal record => rule D (FAILED
         INCOMPLETE_AFTER_RESTART appended; relaunch refused); (iv)
         durability sequence asserted (O_APPEND, fsync calls observed).
     T-3 Reference isolation: with a stub in place of the Reference
         executable, assert the launched argv contains only 9.8(b)
         inputs, the environment is minimal, no Candidate path is
         present in argv/env/cwd, and Reference output paths are
         absent before launch.
     T-4 Reference role sequencing: GATES_PASS then INVOKED durably
         appended BEFORE the subprocess spawn call; COMPLETED/FAILED
         appended after exit; run_id consistent.
     T-5 G1 threshold source: synthetic depth_imbalance_l1 containing
         exact zeros so that T0 and TZ thresholds differ; assert the
         G1 row calculated_threshold equals v1_2_candidate.tz_threshold
         and the (depth_imbalance_l1,TZ,q) HZ row, not the T0 row.
     T-6 fair_gap_reversion: synthetic gap with zeros; assert TZ-row
         calculated_threshold == T0-row calculated_threshold (zero-
         included) while hz_discrimination_class == HZ_DISCRIMINATING
         (diagnostic operand differs); and C1 threshold equals it.
     T-7 mean_abs_move: identical across all HG/HC/HB rows sharing
         (session,asset,horizon); non-NULL for a row with accepted_n=0
         when the A003 domain is non-empty; NULL when that domain is
         empty.
     T-8 Evidence: schema exactness (member set, types, ordering),
         record order = canonical, B1 pre_overlap_positions null,
         parent_positions only for G1/C1, sealing values equal to file
         hash/size and to manifest block and ledger; S1 ignores it; S2
         consumes it and fails on a tampered accepted_positions.
     T-9 S1 lexical: "1000.0" in an integer field => INVALID_INPUT;
         "0.8" vs "0.80" quantile key lexemes => key mismatch (no
         numeric normalization).

13.5 FOCUSED SYNTHETIC TEST DELTA (R1-R4; DRAFT3 addition; added to the
     existing synthetic-test contract of 13.1-13.4 under the same
     constraints: no real NEW36 quantitative byte, no network, no real
     DB, no path under 14.4; synthetic runs do NOT consume NEW36):
     R1-TEST   Reference isolation. With a stub in place of the
               Reference executable, assert the stub receives no
               Candidate artifact (CSV, manifest, evidence, S1/S2
               report, ledger) via argv, stdin, environment variables,
               config/input parameters, explicit path arguments or
               imported data/module dependencies; mere filesystem
               visibility of Candidate artifacts under the synthetic
               cwd is EXPECTED and MUST NOT be treated as a violation
               (9.8(b)).
     R2-TEST   Candidate FAILED gate. With a synthetic Candidate forced
               to FAILED, assert the Reference `preflight`/`generate`
               is never called (no subprocess spawn) and no
               {role:REFERENCE, state:INVOKED} record is appended
               (9.7 step 6).
     R3-TEST-A Recovery reuse. With a synthetic Candidate COMPLETED and
               the Reference not yet INVOKED, simulate an Orchestrator
               restart; assert the SAME run_id is reused for the
               remainder of the attempt (9.11 rule 2).
     R3-TEST-B Cross-role run_id mismatch. With synthetic Candidate and
               Reference COMPLETED ledger records carrying different
               run_id values, assert fail-closed LEDGER_CORRUPTION
               before S1 is invoked (9.10, 9.11 rule 6).
     R3-TEST-C INVOKED-then-restart. With a synthetic Candidate INVOKED
               record and a simulated restart before any terminal
               record, assert recovery appends FAILED with detail
               "INCOMPLETE_AFTER_RESTART" under the SAME run_id (9.9
               rule D, 9.11 rule 5) and that the Reference remains
               uninvoked.
     R4-TEST   Output creation semantics. Assert: the shared
               environment record is created exclusively when absent
               and, on a second synthetic run, is re-validated and
               reused unchanged without being overwritten; the ledger
               is opened/appended with O_APPEND semantics and an
               existing valid synthetic ledger is accepted for
               recovery without truncation; every IMMUTABLE
               EXCLUSIVE-CREATE artifact of 14.4 rejects pre-existence
               ("xb" PRECONDITION_FAIL).

===== BEGIN backend/recovery/v1_2_candidate.py =====
"""RECONSTRUCTION_V1.2 frozen candidate semantics (PRE-NEW36 technical freeze).

This module represents the minimum executable contract for the frozen V1.2
candidate:

    HZ = TZ
    HG = G1        (gate feature = bitget_depth_imbalance_l1)
    HC = C1        (gap feature  = bitget_gap_to_fair_bps)
    HB = B0        (greedy overlap filter with >= spacing)
    G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE = TZ

Hard safety invariants (never remove):

* No import-time execution.
* No golden artifact reads, imports, opens, or path references.
* No NEW36 quantitative artifact access.
* No modification of engine.py, Stage1, Stage2, Stage3, v1_2_diagnostics.
* No side effects at import.
* FrozenAnalysisEngine remains NOT_CONFIGURED / accepts_input=False.

All numerical semantics defer to the frozen engine helpers where present
(:func:`engine._quantile_type7`, :func:`engine._greedy_overlap_filter`,
:func:`engine._build_forward_return_array`). This module intentionally
re-exposes only the *raw-sign, subset-preserving* composition specific to
the frozen V1.2 candidate.

Spec document:  backend/recovery/specs/V1_2_CANDIDATE_SPEC.txt
Acceptance:     backend/recovery/specs/V1_2_ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA.txt
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Final

import numpy as np

from .engine import (
    GRID_MS,
    _greedy_overlap_filter,
    _quantile_type7,
)

CANDIDATE_VERSION: Final[str] = "V1_2_CANDIDATE_FROZEN"

# Frozen axis choices
HZ_VARIANT: Final[str] = "TZ"
HG_VARIANT: Final[str] = "G1"
HC_VARIANT: Final[str] = "C1"
HB_VARIANT: Final[str] = "B0"
G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE: Final[str] = "TZ"

# Frozen constants
THRESHOLD_EXACTNESS_TOLERANCE: Final[float] = 1e-12
CONTROL_ENVELOPE_MULTIPLIER: Final[float] = 1.0

# Frozen columns (from V1.1 FEATURE_MAP)
G1_GATE_FEATURE_COLUMN: Final[str] = "bitget_depth_imbalance_l1"
G1_EXT_OFI_COLUMN: Final[str] = "external_ofi_consensus_l1"
C1_GAP_FEATURE_COLUMN: Final[str] = "bitget_gap_to_fair_bps"

# Explicitly forbidden columns inside G1 / C1 confirmation logic.
CANDIDATE_FORBIDDEN_CONFIRMATION_COLUMNS: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
    "z_di_l1",
    "z_ext_ofi",
    "z_gap_d",
)


# =====================================================================
# TZ threshold (HZ = TZ)
# =====================================================================

def tz_threshold(quality: np.ndarray, signal: np.ndarray, q: float) -> float | None:
    """Compute the frozen HZ=TZ threshold for a raw simple feature.

    Domain:
        quality AND isfinite(signal) AND signal != 0.0

    Threshold:
        type7_quantile( abs(signal), q )

    Returns ``None`` if fewer than 2 finite non-zero values are present in
    the TZ domain (mirroring the frozen V1.1 threshold-None policy which
    forces N=0 downstream).
    """
    if quality.shape != signal.shape:
        raise ValueError(
            f"tz_threshold: shape mismatch quality={quality.shape} "
            f"signal={signal.shape}"
        )
    if not (0.0 < q < 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"tz_threshold: q must be in (0,1); got {q!r}")
    domain = (
        np.asarray(quality, dtype=bool)
        & np.isfinite(signal)
        & (signal != 0.0)
    )
    vals = np.abs(np.asarray(signal, dtype=float)[domain])
    return _quantile_type7(vals, q)


# =====================================================================
# G1 pre-overlap mask (HG = G1 with G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE = TZ)
# =====================================================================

def g1_pre_overlap_mask(
    *,
    quality: np.ndarray,
    di_l1: np.ndarray,
    ext_ofi: np.ndarray,
    fwd: np.ndarray,
    tz_thr: float | None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the boolean pre-overlap event mask for the frozen G1 variant.

    Composition (raw signs only, no z-score confirmation):
        quality
        AND isfinite(di_l1)
        AND di_l1 != 0
        AND abs(di_l1) >= tz_thr
        AND isfinite(ext_ofi)
        AND ext_ofi != 0
        AND sign(ext_ofi) == sign(di_l1)
        AND isfinite(fwd)

    ``tz_thr`` is the TZ threshold of ``depth_imbalance_l1`` for the same
    (session, asset, q) block. If ``tz_thr`` is ``None`` the frozen policy
    forces N=0 (empty mask).
    """
    for name, arr in (("quality", quality), ("di_l1", di_l1),
                      ("ext_ofi", ext_ofi), ("fwd", fwd)):
        if arr.shape != quality.shape:
            raise ValueError(
                f"g1_pre_overlap_mask: {name} shape mismatch {arr.shape} "
                f"vs {quality.shape}"
            )
    if tz_thr is None:
        return np.zeros(quality.shape, dtype=bool)
    q = np.asarray(quality, dtype=bool)
    di = np.asarray(di_l1, dtype=float)
    ofi = np.asarray(ext_ofi, dtype=float)
    fw = np.asarray(fwd, dtype=float)
    return (
        q
        & np.isfinite(di) & (di != 0.0)
        & (np.abs(di) >= float(tz_thr))
        & np.isfinite(ofi) & (ofi != 0.0)
        & (np.sign(ofi) == np.sign(di))
        & np.isfinite(fw)
    )


def g1_parent_gate_mask(
    *,
    quality: np.ndarray,
    di_l1: np.ndarray,
    fwd: np.ndarray,
    tz_thr: float | None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the parent depth_imbalance_l1 gate mask against which G1 must
    be a subset (I_G1_PARENT_SUBSET).

    Composition:
        quality AND isfinite(di_l1) AND di_l1!=0
        AND abs(di_l1) >= tz_thr AND isfinite(fwd)
    """
    if tz_thr is None:
        return np.zeros(quality.shape, dtype=bool)
    q = np.asarray(quality, dtype=bool)
    di = np.asarray(di_l1, dtype=float)
    fw = np.asarray(fwd, dtype=float)
    return (
        q
        & np.isfinite(di) & (di != 0.0)
        & (np.abs(di) >= float(tz_thr))
        & np.isfinite(fw)
    )


def assert_g1_parent_subset(
    g1_mask: np.ndarray, parent_mask: np.ndarray
) -> None:
    """Fail-closed subset invariant: G1 pre-overlap events must be a subset
    of the parent depth_l1 gate. Raises ``AssertionError`` on violation.
    """
    if g1_mask.shape != parent_mask.shape:
        raise AssertionError(
            f"I_G1_PARENT_SUBSET FAIL: shape mismatch "
            f"g1={g1_mask.shape} parent={parent_mask.shape}"
        )
    violations = np.asarray(g1_mask, dtype=bool) & (
        ~np.asarray(parent_mask, dtype=bool)
    )
    if bool(violations.any()):
        idxs = np.nonzero(violations)[0][:8].tolist()
        raise AssertionError(
            f"I_G1_PARENT_SUBSET FAIL: {int(violations.sum())} G1 events "
            f"outside parent gate; first_indices={idxs}"
        )


def g1_direction(di_l1: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Frozen G1 direction: sign(di_l1). Zero rows are already excluded by
    the pre-overlap mask; caller must apply mask before using direction."""
    return np.sign(np.asarray(di_l1, dtype=float))


# =====================================================================
# C1 pre-overlap mask (HC = C1, raw signs only)
# =====================================================================

def c1_pre_overlap_mask(
    *,
    quality: np.ndarray,
    gap: np.ndarray,
    di_l1: np.ndarray,
    ext_ofi: np.ndarray,
    fwd: np.ndarray,
    gap_thr: float | None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the boolean pre-overlap event mask for the frozen C1 variant.

    Composition (raw signs only, no z-score confirmation):
        quality
        AND isfinite(gap) AND gap != 0
        AND abs(gap) >= gap_thr
        AND isfinite(di_l1) AND di_l1 != 0
        AND sign(di_l1) == -sign(gap)
        AND isfinite(ext_ofi) AND ext_ofi != 0
        AND sign(ext_ofi) == -sign(gap)
        AND isfinite(fwd)

    ``gap_thr`` is the frozen fair-gap threshold (unchanged by HZ zero rule).
    ``None`` forces N=0.
    """
    for name, arr in (("quality", quality), ("gap", gap),
                      ("di_l1", di_l1), ("ext_ofi", ext_ofi),
                      ("fwd", fwd)):
        if arr.shape != quality.shape:
            raise ValueError(
                f"c1_pre_overlap_mask: {name} shape mismatch {arr.shape} "
                f"vs {quality.shape}"
            )
    if gap_thr is None:
        return np.zeros(quality.shape, dtype=bool)
    q = np.asarray(quality, dtype=bool)
    g = np.asarray(gap, dtype=float)
    di = np.asarray(di_l1, dtype=float)
    ofi = np.asarray(ext_ofi, dtype=float)
    fw = np.asarray(fwd, dtype=float)
    sg = np.sign(g)
    return (
        q
        & np.isfinite(g) & (g != 0.0)
        & (np.abs(g) >= float(gap_thr))
        & np.isfinite(di) & (di != 0.0)
        & (np.sign(di) == -sg)
        & np.isfinite(ofi) & (ofi != 0.0)
        & (np.sign(ofi) == -sg)
        & np.isfinite(fw)
    )


def c1_gap_parent_mask(
    *,
    quality: np.ndarray,
    gap: np.ndarray,
    fwd: np.ndarray,
    gap_thr: float | None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the shared gap threshold-crossing set against which C1 must
    be a subset (I_C1_GAP_SUBSET)."""
    if gap_thr is None:
        return np.zeros(quality.shape, dtype=bool)
    q = np.asarray(quality, dtype=bool)
    g = np.asarray(gap, dtype=float)
    fw = np.asarray(fwd, dtype=float)
    return (
        q
        & np.isfinite(g) & (g != 0.0)
        & (np.abs(g) >= float(gap_thr))
        & np.isfinite(fw)
    )


def assert_c1_gap_subset(
    c1_mask: np.ndarray, gap_parent_mask: np.ndarray
) -> None:
    """Fail-closed subset invariant: C1 pre-overlap events must be a subset
    of the shared gap threshold-crossing set.
    """
    if c1_mask.shape != gap_parent_mask.shape:
        raise AssertionError(
            f"I_C1_GAP_SUBSET FAIL: shape mismatch "
            f"c1={c1_mask.shape} parent={gap_parent_mask.shape}"
        )
    violations = np.asarray(c1_mask, dtype=bool) & (
        ~np.asarray(gap_parent_mask, dtype=bool)
    )
    if bool(violations.any()):
        idxs = np.nonzero(violations)[0][:8].tolist()
        raise AssertionError(
            f"I_C1_GAP_SUBSET FAIL: {int(violations.sum())} C1 events "
            f"outside gap parent set; first_indices={idxs}"
        )


def c1_direction(gap: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Frozen C1 direction: -sign(gap)."""
    return -np.sign(np.asarray(gap, dtype=float))


# =====================================================================
# HB filters (B0 greedy = candidate; B1 strict = predeclared diagnostic)
# =====================================================================

def spacing_steps_for_horizon(horizon_ms: int) -> int:
    """Frozen spacing_steps = max(10, horizon_ms // GRID_MS)."""
    if horizon_ms <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"horizon_ms must be positive; got {horizon_ms!r}")
    k = int(horizon_ms) // int(GRID_MS)
    return max(10, k)


def b0_greedy_filter(
    positions: np.ndarray, spacing_steps: int
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Frozen candidate greedy filter.

    Accepts the first event; subsequently accepts iff
    ``pos - last_accepted >= spacing_steps`` (equality accepted).

    Positions must be sorted ascending. Returns a boolean mask.
    """
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=np.int64)
    if pos.ndim != 1:
        raise ValueError("b0_greedy_filter: positions must be 1-D")
    if pos.size > 1 and bool(np.any(pos[1:] < pos[:-1])):
        raise ValueError("b0_greedy_filter: positions must be sorted ascending")
    return _greedy_overlap_filter(pos, int(spacing_steps))


def b1_diagnostic_filter(
    positions: np.ndarray, spacing_steps: int
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Predeclared HB diagnostic filter using strict '>' spacing.

    Never a candidate. Provided solely for the future acceptance evaluator
    to compute discriminating cells (Section G).
    """
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=np.int64)
    if pos.ndim != 1:
        raise ValueError("b1_diagnostic_filter: positions must be 1-D")
    if pos.size > 1 and bool(np.any(pos[1:] < pos[:-1])):
        raise ValueError("b1_diagnostic_filter: positions must be sorted ascending")
    accepted = np.zeros(pos.size, dtype=bool)
    last = -int(spacing_steps) - 1
    s = int(spacing_steps)
    for i in range(pos.size):
        p = int(pos[i])
        if p - last > s:  # strict >
            accepted[i] = True
            last = p
    return accepted


def exact_spacing_pair_n(positions: np.ndarray, spacing_steps: int) -> int:
    """Count of consecutive-in-input position pairs whose gap == spacing_steps.

    A discriminating HB cell requires ``exact_spacing_pair_n > 0`` AND
    B0/B1 accepted-position digests to differ.
    """
    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=np.int64)
    if pos.size < 2:
        return 0
    return int(np.sum((pos[1:] - pos[:-1]) == int(spacing_steps)))


def assert_hb_i11(b0_accepted_n: int, b1_accepted_n: int) -> None:
    """I_HB_I11: B1.accepted_n <= B0.accepted_n per paired case."""
    if int(b1_accepted_n) > int(b0_accepted_n):
        raise AssertionError(
            f"I_HB_I11 FAIL: B1.accepted_n={b1_accepted_n} > "
            f"B0.accepted_n={b0_accepted_n}"
        )

__all__ = [
    "CANDIDATE_VERSION",
    "HZ_VARIANT", "HG_VARIANT", "HC_VARIANT", "HB_VARIANT",
    "G1_THRESHOLD_SOURCE",
    "THRESHOLD_EXACTNESS_TOLERANCE",
    "CONTROL_ENVELOPE_MULTIPLIER",
    "G1_GATE_FEATURE_COLUMN", "G1_EXT_OFI_COLUMN", "C1_GAP_FEATURE_COLUMN",
    "CANDIDATE_FORBIDDEN_CONFIRMATION_COLUMNS",
    "tz_threshold",
    "g1_pre_overlap_mask", "g1_parent_gate_mask",
    "assert_g1_parent_subset", "g1_direction",
    "c1_pre_overlap_mask", "c1_gap_parent_mask",
    "assert_c1_gap_subset", "c1_direction",
    "spacing_steps_for_horizon",
    "b0_greedy_filter", "b1_diagnostic_filter",
    "exact_spacing_pair_n",
    "assert_hb_i11",
]
===== END backend/recovery/v1_2_candidate.py =====
